It then materializes the generated React/Vite project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and DOM, and executes Playwright programs. During execution it collects visual evidence, DOM states, network responses, and persistent-state observations, which are used for per-flow judgments. The grader is improved before RL through an offline self-evolution loop. The authors build a controlled environment with clean applications and source-level single-fault variants, run a base verifier, attribute residual errors to stages such as planning, grounding, evidence collection, or judgment, and mutate only the corresponding skills. Promoted skills are organized in a SkillGraph with dependency, composition, and conflict relations. This SkillGraph is frozen before policy training, where WebGrader supplies functional rewards for GRPO training of an 8B policy.3d:T445,The verifier environment, WebGen-Verifier-100, contains 100 public WebGen-Instruct training queries across 10 application categories. Each query is paired with a locally verified clean React/Vite application. From each clean application, the authors create eight natural source-level single-fault variants by modifying localized business logic. Out of 800 materialized faults, 778 remain active. The page-level split is 60/20/20 for Train/Eval/Test, with 461/157/160 active faults and 282/95/93 active flows. Clean applications, their variants, and private references do not cross splits. Train is used for residual induction and mutation, Eval for candidate promotion, and Test only for reported verifier evaluation. For policy training, the WebGrader-trained 8B model improves over a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 FSR points and outperforms o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash on WebGen-Bench and WG-core-250. The provided text does not include the full result tables, so exact verifier precision, failure modes, and per-skill ablations should be checked in the experiment section.3e:T899,方法上，WebRider的关键设计是把“长期委托控制”和“当前页面动作实现”硬性拆开，而不是让一个单体 LLM 在同一上下文里同时负责计划、证据判断、动作生成和终止决策。顶层 controller 维护一个显式状态，里面包含 goal、hard constraints、soft preferences、blocking conditions、evidence ledger、progress 和 next intention。它只负责高层决策：继续浏览、发起澄清、停止、标记外部阻塞、还是给出答案；如果继续浏览，它会发出一个紧凑的 intention，例如“核验该商品的退货政策”或“为兼容性再找一个来源”。

中层不允许判断“证据是否足够”或“是否可以最终回答”，它只根据当前 observation、顶层 intention、persona state 和近期历史，生成一个且仅一个可执行的 JSON Action AST，动作空间包括 Click、Type、Scroll、Search、Maps、Back、Wait 以及安全回退。这里的重点不是让模型自由生成文本动作，而是通过确定性的 compiler 和 guard 把动作约束成可执行、可校验的局部目标：语法错误、非法 target、重复状态、阻塞状态都会被拦截，并附带恢复动作。训练时监督的不是原始模型文本，而是 guard 之后最终真正执行的 action。这样做的好处是，中层 policy 的学习目标被压缩成一个局部、可验证、可替换的 action realization 问题，而 CAPTCHA、访问策略、证据充分性、最终回答等复杂控制仍由 guard 或顶层负责。

另一条主线是 persona-conditioned intent contract。作者强调 persona 不是人口统计属性，也不是长期记忆，而是任务局部的控制策略，决定证据收集、排序偏好、不确定性处理和 stopping 规则。contract 由 goal、硬约束、软偏好、阻塞条件、证据义务、答案 schema 等组成，并且除非用户显式澄清，否则在执行期间保持稳定。这个设计让同一个 request 在固定 hard constraints 下可以配不同 persona，形成 counterfactual setting，用来测“同一个任务，不同委托 policy 下 agent 是否真的走了不同且正确的路径”。3f:T8c4,The main design choice is a hard separation between long-horizon delegation control and page-local action realization. Instead of letting one monolithic LLM jointly decide plans, evidence sufficiency, browser actions, and stopping, WebRider uses a hierarchy.

The top layer maintains an explicit state containing the goal, hard constraints, soft preferences, blocking conditions, evidence ledger, progress, and next intention. It decides whether to continue browsing, ask for clarification, stop, flag an external blocker, or answer. If browsing continues, it emits a compact intention such as “verify the return policy for this candidate” or “find a second source for compatibility.”

The middle layer is deliberately narrower. Given the current observation, the top-layer intention, persona state, and recent history, it emits exactly one JSON Action AST from a constrained action space: Click, Type, Scroll, Search, Maps, Back, Wait, or a safe fallback. Crucially, it is not allowed to decide whether evidence is sufficient or whether the task is ready for final answer. A deterministic compiler plus guards enforce executability: malformed targets, invalid terminals, repeated states, and blocked states are intercepted, with recovery actions attached when needed. Training supervision is defined on the final post-guard executable action, not on raw model text. That turns the middle policy into a local, verifiable action-realization problem, while harder issues such as CAPTCHA, access policy, evidence sufficiency, and final answering remain with the guards or the top layer.

The second core contribution is the persona-conditioned intent contract. Persona here is not demographic identity or long-term memory; it is a task-local control policy governing evidence gathering, uncertainty handling, ranking, interaction, and stopping. The contract includes the goal, hard constraints, soft preferences, blockers, evidence obligations, and answer schema, and remains stable unless the user explicitly clarifies. This lets the benchmark create counterfactual variants of the same request under different personas, so the evaluation can test whether the agent truly follows different delegated policies under fixed hard constraints.40:T6ab,The most informative part of the empirical section is the evaluation design and the failure decomposition, not model scaling. The paper introduces RiderBench, expanding 768 audited requests into 4,096 persona-conditioned contracts across 42 sites. The setup supports paired comparisons: the same request can be evaluated under different personas while keeping hard constraints and evidence obligations fixed.

The key result is the gap between completion and fidelity. The strong baseline controller, Full-Pro, terminates on 99.2% of contracts, but only 38.8% pass all contract gates. In other words, finishing the task and honoring the delegated policy are very different things. The paired human study reinforces this point: final-trace success and stepwise acceptability diverge significantly. A trajectory may look acceptable from the final answer alone, yet fail under stepwise audit because it skipped required evidence checks, failed to ask when uncertainty should have triggered clarification, or violated the persona’s risk policy.

The mechanism studies focus on persistent intention. The paper compares settings such as persona-only prompting, intention text alone, and explicit persistent intention state, and finds that maintaining intention as a state variable matters more than merely including persona text in the prompt. Another useful result concerns trainability of the middle layer: because each step yields a guarded executable action as a local target, the middle policy can be trained and swapped while holding the top controller, guards, and executor fixed. That makes the proposal more than an evaluation wrapper; it is also a modular training interface for process-level control.41:T52f,Start with the Introduction and Figure 1. The central claim is that web-agent evaluation is missing delegation fidelity, not just task success. Then read the Hierarchical Controller section carefully. The key question is why the top and middle layers must be structurally separated, and why supervision is placed on post-guard executable actions rather than free-form action text. That is the architectural core.

Next, read the intent contract section with attention to what is stable and what can change only through explicit user clarification. The paper is trying to prevent policy drift during execution, so the stability of the contract is not a minor detail.

In the experiments, prioritize the RiderBench construction, the definition of contract gates, the 99.2% vs 38.8% result, and the paired human study. Those pieces establish why endpoint-only evaluation is insufficient. If time remains, read the ablations on persistent intention and the analysis of guarded middle traces. Those sections determine whether the work is merely a benchmark proposal or a meaningful training interface for agent control.

A reasonable skim takes about 25–35 minutes. For a pretraining-oriented reader, the main value is not browser mechanics but how the supervision target is shifted from final answers to structured process state.42:T907,MemPrism explicitly separates persistent memory from decision-time working memory. The framework has five modules: Recorder, Router, Composer, Render, and Adapter. In inference, the first four form the main pipeline. Recorder stores real interactions as a unified event stream, keeping both raw events and structured fields such as pre-action observation, executed action, environment feedback, reward or step label, and task metadata. The point is to keep persistent memory neutral and reusable rather than collapsing it early into one fixed summary.

Router is the key learnable component. Instead of directly predicting the next environment action, it first decides how history should be viewed. A view action is defined over several axes: relation type, evidence range, outcome condition, and granularity. In other words, the policy chooses what relational structure to build, how much history to include, whether to filter by success/failure or other outcomes, and how coarse or fine the representation should be. This makes read-time memory representation an explicit decision variable.

Composer then selects events from the event stream and assembles a relational structure according to the chosen view action. Render converts that structure into an optical working-memory view. The optical format is not used only for token compression. The authors use two-dimensional layout to encode relations directly: spatial proximity groups related events, row/column alignment supports state comparison, arrows indicate change or dependency, and highlights emphasize failures or task-relevant evidence. A frozen task policy consumes the current input together with this temporary view and outputs the next environment action. The view is discarded after the step and is never written back into persistent memory, which avoids irreversible distortion from fixed summaries or repeated compression.

Training is also split cleanly. Router is first initialized with action-conditioned soft supervision, then optimized with trajectory-level grouped GRPO. The learning target is not the environment action itself, but the choice of memory view that best supports downstream action selection. This separation helps attribute gains to the memory representation interface rather than to a jointly entangled agent policy.43:T6a7,From the provided full-text excerpt, the experimental emphasis is on whether task-conditioned view selection improves long-horizon decision making, not on raw storage capacity. The paper appears to provide two kinds of evidence: end-task gains and attribution studies showing that the gains come from working-memory construction rather than unrelated components. The training setup for Router—soft supervision followed by grouped GRPO—is already designed to isolate the effect of view selection.

The excerpt does not include full tables or final numbers, so the exact quantitative gains cannot be stated reliably here. Still, the intended comparisons are clear: fixed representations such as timelines, summaries, or flat retrieved snippets are contrasted with dynamically constructed relational views defined by relation type, evidence range, outcome filter, and granularity. The expectation is that dynamic views are better matched to subtasks like loop detection, state tracking, failure analysis, and dependency reasoning.

Another likely experimental point is the decision not to write temporary views back into persistent memory. The authors argue that writing them back would bake task-local bias into long-term memory and reduce future reuse. A meaningful ablation should therefore compare persistent event streams plus on-demand views against fixed summary memories or recursively compressed memories. When reading the experiments, the most useful things to look for are: whether Router’s chosen view distribution changes with task state, how large the gap is between fixed and dynamic views, and whether optical views help because they expose structure rather than merely saving tokens.44:T5cc,Start with the Introduction and make sure the distinction between representation mismatch, storage failure, and retrieval failure is clear. That problem decomposition is the foundation of the whole paper. Without it, the method can look like another memory-engineering variant.

Then read the Method section with focus on the five-module pipeline, especially Router’s action space. Two questions matter: why should view selection be modeled explicitly instead of being left implicit inside the main policy prompt, and why should temporary working views never be written back into persistent memory? The first is about a learnable memory interface; the second is about preventing task-local bias from contaminating long-term memory.

Next, inspect the optical-view examples carefully. The important point is not just “history as images to save tokens.” The stronger claim is that 2D layout can expose relational structure directly. If the paper includes visual examples, they are worth reading closely because they reveal whether the method truly changes information organization or merely changes modality.

In the experiments, prioritize the training of the view policy, comparisons against fixed views, and ablations over relation types. A practical skim should take about 20–30 minutes. For a pretraining-oriented reader, the main takeaway is the interface question: how the same evidence can be exposed to a model in different structures depending on the decision Reliable estimates would therefore allow environment designers to calibrate evaluation benchmarks and construct progressive training curricula. This becomes increasingly important as agents move into long-horizon domains, where empirical trial-and-error is a severe computational bottlene","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T09:33:09Z","category_id":"scaling","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05797.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个评测与训练管线里的前置问题：在不做 rollouts、不运行完整 agent 交互的前提下，仅根据任务描述和少量静态元信息，能否预估一个 agent 任务有多难。作者关心的不是单个模型的 uncertainty 校准，而是跨 benchmark、跨 agent 配置地定义“任务难度”这一潜变量，并检验这种 ex ante 预测能否替代昂贵的实测试跑。问题之所以重要，是因为长时程 agent benchmark 的验证成本越来越高，很多任务一次评测就要消耗大量环境交互和模型调用；如果难度不能预估，benchmark 构造、课程学习和数据筛选都会变得很低效。","importance":"对预训练研究的关联不是直接改进 base model，而是提供了一个和数据课程、评测设计、合成任务筛选都相关的工具视角。第一，它把 task difficulty 从“经验上觉得难”变成了可估计、可排序的量，这对构造 curriculum、挑选训练样本、控制 benchmark 覆盖度有现实意义。第二，论文提醒人们不要用 response-level AUC 这类看起来很高的指标误判难度预测能力，因为它可能只是在区分成功/失败样本，而没有真正学到 task-level 难度排序。第三，作者公开了一个跨 17 个 agent benchmark 的结果库，并用 IRT 建 difficulty target，这对后续做 agent scaling、benchmark contamination 分析、任务去重和难度分层采样都有参考价值。它更像一篇评测方法与数据资产论文，而不是预训练算法论文。","technical_approach":"方法分两步。第一步，从已有 agent-task 成败记录中估计每个任务的 latent difficulty。作者把每次 trial 视作 Bernoulli 观测，构造一个部分观测的 agent×task 二值响应矩阵，然后用 1PL IRT（Rasch model）拟合每个 agent 的能力参数 θ_a 和每个任务的难度参数 β_t。这样定义难度有一个好处：它不是某个单一模型的通过率，而是在共享标尺上相对于多种 agent 能力共同估出来的，因此更适合跨 benchmark 比较。\n\n第二步，用 rollout 前可获得的特征去预测 β_t。特征包括任务文本描述、环境与动作空间描述、元数据，以及作者特别强调的 entropy 等不需要完整交互即可得到的信号。论文还系统讨论了评估协议：如果用 response AUC，模型可能在样本级别上看起来预测得不错，但 task-level rank correlation 仍然很弱，因此作者主张用 Spearman ρ 这类排序指标来评估“是否真的学到了难度”。此外，论文做了 residual diagnostics：把预测难度与 IRT 估计难度的残差拿出来分析。正残差大的任务，往往是比文本表面看起来更难，原因可能是环境不可行、verifier 有问题、接口设计不合理；负残差大的任务，则可能因为训练数据熟悉、模板化、捷径存在而显得异常容易。这个诊断视角比单纯做一个回归器更有用。","experiments":"数据规模是这篇论文最扎实的部分。主分析子集包含 17 个 benchmark、5230 个任务、497 个 agent 配置，这些配置来自 216 个模型和 90 个 scaffold；总语料库包含 415,470 条 agent-task outcome。任务类型覆盖 coding/SWE、function calling、web navigation、computer use、terminal、math、ML、cybersecurity 和 general-purpose suites，时间跨度从 2010 到 2026。每个 agent-task 对平均约 1.2 次 trial。\n\n结果上，作者给出的核心数字是：在 K-fold 评估下，组合特征的 task difficulty 预测达到 Spearman ρ=0.399，且相对 baseline 的提升有统计显著性（p<0.001）；在 out-of-distribution 设置下，ρ=0.225。单一特征族里，entropy 最强。论文还指出 response AUC 可能偏乐观，即便 task difficulty 排序学得不好，AUC 仍然可以很高，因此 rank-based 指标更可信。更有信息量的是残差分析案例：一些任务被模型预测为不难，但真实 IRT 难度很高，最后发现是环境不可完成或 evaluator 失灵；另一些任务文本上看似复杂，实际却因数据熟悉或 shortcut 而容易。这说明 difficulty predictor 不只是一个打分器，还能帮助发现 benchmark 设计缺陷。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看数据与目标定义，也就是 17 个 benchmark 如何统一成 agent×task 响应矩阵，以及为什么作者选 1PL IRT 而不是直接用平均成功率。这里决定了整篇论文是否站得住。然后看评估部分，重点理解作者为什么批评 response AUC，并把 task-level rank correlation 作为主指标。接着看 feature ablation，尤其是 entropy 为什么在单特征里表现最好，以及组合特征带来了什么增益。最后看 residual diagnostics 的案例分析，这部分最接近实际使用场景：它告诉你难度预测器能否帮助发现坏任务、脏 verifier 和 shortcut。预计 25–35 分钟可以读完主线；如果只想抓结论，看 §3 的 IRT 建模、§4/§5 using only task descriptions and static metadata, instead of running expensive agent-environment interactions? The target is not model-specific uncertainty calibration, but a benchmark-level notion of latent task difficulty that transfers across agent configurations and benchmark families. This matters because long-horizon agent benchmarks are becoming costly to validate, and without ex ante difficulty estimates it is hard to build calibrated evaluations, progressive curricula, or efficient task filters.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is indirect but real. Difficulty estimation can support curriculum construction, synthetic task selection, benchmark curation, and contamination analysis. The paper makes three useful points. First, it turns task difficulty from an informal intuition into an estimable latent variable that can be ranked across tasks. Second, it shows that response-level AUC can be misleadingly optimistic: a predictor may separate easy and hard responses without learning the actual ordering of task difficulty. Third, it releases a large multi-benchmark corpus and uses IRT-derived difficulty targets, which should be useful for future work on agent scaling, benchmark design, and difficulty-aware sampling. We show that on reasoning tasks this freedom is instead the axis of failure. Logging every commitment during decoding of LLaDA-8B on GSM8K, we find that unconstrained (pure) decoding commits the final answer at 15-24% of the trajectory while half the reasoning region is still masked, and collapses to answer-only outputs on up to 90% of problems as th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T07:25:04Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05687.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 masked diffusion LLM 在推理任务上的解码顺序问题。dLLM 可以任意顺序提交 token，这通常被视为相对自回归模型的优势；但在 GSM8K、MATH-500 这类需要 chain-of-thought 的任务上，无约束 confidence-ordered decoding 会过早提交答案位置，甚至直接提交 EOS 和 padding，导致模型在推理区域仍大面积 masked 时已经固定最终答案。","importance":"这是一篇对 diffusion LLM 训练和推理都有启发的工作。它把 dLLM 推理失败从“模型想不想推理”拆成“采样器允许它先提交哪里”。结论很具体：问题不主要来自 EOS belief，而来自 reachability，即 sampler 能否在很远的位置立即提交 EOS 或最终答案。这个区分重要，因为它说明部分失败可以通过解码约束修复，不一定需要重新训练；也说明任意顺序生成并不天然适合推理，commitment order 是 dLLM scaling、RL 训练和 CoT 数据设计里需要显式控制的变量。","technical_approach":"论文没有提出新模型，而是对 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 的 denoising 过程做细粒度记录：每个位置何时从 mask 变成确定 token，每一步该位置的 top prediction 是什么。基于这些日志，作者区分了两类失败。第一类是 answer-only collapse：模型跳过推理，输出短答案后接 padding；canvas 越长，远处 EOS 越容易被提前提交。第二类是 answer-first backfill：即便最后有推理文本，答案也在轨迹前 1/4 左右被冻结，推理 token 的中位提交时间接近轨迹中点，后续推导只能围绕已经固定的答案补写。论文进一步比较 pure decoding 与 semi-AR/block-wise decoding，发现二者的 EOS pressure 几乎相同，但 EOS commitment 差异超过 2 倍。因此，差异不在模型对 EOS 的预测倾向，而在无约束 sampler 是否能对远处位置采取行动。干预方法是 window-restricted decoding：只允许提交 left-most masked position 之后 w 个位置内的 token。w=∞ 对应 pure decoding，w=1 接近左到右生成；这个单一参数直接限制远距离提交能力，不改 logits，也不重新训练。","experiments":"主要实验使用 LLaDA-8B-Instruct。GSM8K 上，无约束 pure decoding 在 256-token canvas 时超过三分之一问题出现 answer-only collapse；canvas 扩到 512 时，collapse 最高达到约 90%，说明更大的生成空间反而让模型更容易跳过推理。答案提交时间也显著早于推理文本：最终答案通常在 denoising 轨迹前 1/4 固定，而推理区 token 的中位提交时间接近一半。论文用 2×2 设计交叉 prompt 类型和 decoder 类型：prompt 分为要求 CoT 与只要答案，decoder 分为 pure 与 semi-AR。在 250 个成对问题上，prompt–decoder interaction 约 35 个百分点，permutation p<1e-4。answer-only 条件下两种 decoder 统计上接近；要求 CoT 时，semi-AR 获得约 31 个百分点收益，而 pure decoding 几乎不能把 CoT 转化为同等性能。这个结果说明 semi-AR 的优势主要来自推理文本真正发挥作用，而不是答案格式或任务先验。论文还在 Dream-7B 和 MATH-500 上复现了模式；MATH-500 中短答案难以猜测，collapse 减少，但 answer-first 的 order channel 仍存在。窗口约束的效果很直接：在 LLaDA-8B GSM8K 上，所有 w≤64 都能把 pure decoding 从 0.528 提升到 0.82–0.85，接近 semi-AR；滑动窗口 w=32 与 block size 32 的结果相差约 1 个点，说明有效因素是 reachability 限制，而不一定是固定 block 结构。代价也被测量了：在完整 refinement 下，w=1 最优，任意顺序没有带来精度收益；当每步提交约 8 个 token 时，约束反而有害。有效区间内，gated decoder 仍可保持接近 AR 的准确率，并报告约 4× wall-clock speedup。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Introduction 中对 answer-only collapse、answer-first backfill、EOS pressure 与 reachability 的区分，这是论文的主线。随后重点读日志分析章节，尤其是 token commitment time 的分布、答案位置与推理区域的提交顺序，以及 EOS pressure/commitment 的对照图。2×2 prompt–decoder 实验需要仔细看，因为它回答了 CoT 在 dLLM 中是否只是表面文本的问题；这里的 interaction 和 answer-only near-tie 是关键证据。窗口约束实验也值得细读，尤其是 w 的 sweep、sliding window 与 block-wise decoding 的对照、以及 compute budget 下的精度—速度权衡。对做 diffusion LM 或 masked LM 预训练的人，最值得深挖的是：训练目标是否应该显式学习可用的 commitment order，RL 是否会被 confidence-ordered sampler 限制探索，以及长 canvas 数据中的 EOS/padding 结构如何与采样 reachability 共同作用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies commitment order in masked diffusion LLMs on reasoning tasks. These models can unmask tokens in any order, but unconstrained confidence-ordered decoding often commits the final answer before most reasoning tokens are available, or commits EOS and padding so early that the model produces answer-only outputs.","importance":"This is a useful paper for diffusion-LM research because it separates model beliefs from sampler reachability. The failure is not mainly that the model has stronger EOS beliefs under pure decoding; EOS pressure is nearly decoder-invariant. The failure arises because the sampler is allowed to act on those beliefs at distant positions. This matters for decoding, RL exploration, CoT supervision, and pretraining data design for masked diffusion LMs. Any-order generation is not automatically compatible with multi-step reasoning; commitment order needs to be treated as a controlled variable.","technical_approach":"The work instruments the denoising loop of LLaDA-8B-Instruct. For each position, it logs when the position is committed and what the model predicts at each step. This reveals two distinct pathologies. The first is answer-only collapse: the model emits a bare answer followed by padding, skipping the reasoning region. The second is answer-first backfill: the final answer is fixed early, and the derivation is filled in later around an already committed conclusion. The paper then compares pure decoding with semi-autoregressive or block-wise decoding and shows that EOS pressure is almost the same across decoders, while EOS commitment differs by more than a factor of two. The intervention is window-restricted decoding: only positions within a window of size w after the left-most masked position are eligible for commitment. w=∞ recovers pure decoding, and w=1 approximates left-to-right generation. The method changes only eligibility, not logits or model weights.","experiments":"$d","reading_guide":"This is worth a careful read. These indicate a ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06529.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论 MDLM 中 soft-masking 的反馈表示问题：已有方法用 embedding 空间里的线性插值，把 [MASK] embedding 和 top-k 预测 token embedding 混合，但训练后的 token embedding 更接近等范数球面结构，线性插值会把反馈点推离主要表示流形，导致预测反馈没有被充分利用，甚至带来生成困惑度退化。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发在于，它把 diffusion LM 的一个看似工程化的反馈细节，重新放到表示几何里分析。贡献不是新的扩散目标，而是指出 MDLM 的 denoising feedback 需要尊重 embedding manifold。这个问题在 masked diffusion / any-order LM 中较重要，因为非 AR 模型会反复把中间预测送回 backbone，反馈表示质量直接影响训练动态和采样效率。它也提醒预训练研究者：token embedding 的范数、角度结构不是诊断指标而已，可能决定中间态构造是否合理。","technical_approach":"方法是 Spherical Soft-Masking。原始 soft-masking 先取 top-k token embedding 的概率加权欧氏均值，再用 LERP 和 mask embedding 做 convex blend。S-SM 做两处替换。第一，先把 top-k 候选 embedding 投到单位球面，在球面上求 Fréchet/Karcher mean，避免欧氏均值落到球内。第二，用 SLERP 沿测地线在 mask 方向和预测均值方向之间插值，而不是用直线插值。最后把结果缩放回 mask token 的原始范数，再输入 backbone。训练目标、mask corruption schedule、unmasking rule、confidence weight 的定义都保持不变，因此它是 soft-masking 的几何替换件，而不是一套新的 MDLM 训练框架。","experiments":"论文先在已发布的 MDLM checkpoint 上做几何诊断：归一化后的 mask embedding 与 top-k 预测均值方向之间的测地角在训练中基本稳定在约 73°；token embedding 的 L2 norm 沿 GPT-2 frequency rank 基本平坦。这两个结果支持 hyperspherical geometry 的假设。生成实验中，S-SM 在较大 NFE 预算 256 和 512 下，相比 vanilla MDLM 的 MAUVE 接近翻倍；相比 TopK/LERP，在相同预算下 MAUVE 提升超过 50%。同时，LERP 会带来 generative perplexity 退化，S-SM 能避免这一点。在使用等价 learned confidence 训练方案时，SLERP 在多个 seed 和所有采样预算下取得更高 MAUVE 和更低生成困惑度，而训练困惑度几乎相同。learned confidence weight 也支持这个解释：step 7k 时 SLERP 的 λ 收敛到约 0.056，LERP 约 0.030，前者约为后者 1.9 倍，说明优化器更愿意使用几何上合理的反馈。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 MDLM soft-masking 信息丢失的定义，再看几何诊断部分，重点是 mask-to-prediction angle 和 embedding norm across frequency rank 两个图。方法部分只需确认 Fréchet mean、SLERP、rescale 三步如何替换原 LERP。实验部分优先看 MAUVE、generative perplexity、NFE budget 的对比，以及 learned confidence λ 的消融。值得深挖的是：这个球面假设是否依赖特定 tokenizer、embedding tying、模型规模和训练阶段；如果 diffusion LM 使用 continuous latent 或 adaptive embedding norm，S-SM 的优势可能会变化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a feedback representation issue in masked diffusion language models. Existing soft-masking methods linearly interpolate between the [MASK] embedding and a weighted average of top-k predicted token embeddings. The authors argue that this is mismatched to the learned embedding geometry, which behaves more like a hypersphere than a Euclidean cloud. Linear interpolation then moves the feedback vector off the dominant representation manifold and can weaken or distort predictive feedback.","importance":"The main value is not a new diffusion objective, but a sharper treatment of the intermediate states used by diffusion LMs. MDLMs repeatedly feed partially denoised predictions back into the backbone, so the geometry of those feedback embeddings affects convergence, sample quality, and NFE efficiency. For pretraining researchers, the paper is a useful reminder that embedding geometry can constrain valid design choices in non-autoregressive language modeling, especially when the model consumes its own soft predictions during training or sampling.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, Spherical Soft-Masking, replaces the Euclidean operations in standard soft-masking with intrinsic spherical operations. Instead of taking a probability-weighted linear average of top-k token embeddings, it normalizes candidate embeddings and computes a Fréchet/Karcher mean on the unit sphere. Instead of blending this mean with the mask embedding by LERP, it uses SLERP along the geodesic between the mask direction and the predicted-token mean direction. The resulting vector is then rescaled to the native norm of the mask embedding before being passed into the backbone. The corruption schedule, denoising objective, unmasking policy, and confidence-based mixing mechanism are left unchanged.","experiments":"The empirical motivation comes from geometry probes on a released MDLM checkpoint. The geodesic angle between the normalized mask embedding and the normalized top-k prediction mean stays close to 73 degrees during training, while token embedding norms remain nearly flat across GPT-2 frequency rank. These observations support the hyperspherical view. In generation experiments, S-SM nearly doubles MAUVE over the vanilla MDLM baseline at larger NFE budgets such as 256 and 512, and improves MAUVE by more than 50% over TopK/LERP at the same budgets. It also avoids the generative perplexity degradation observed with LERP. Under an equivalent learned-confidence training setup, SLERP gives higher MAUVE and lower generative perplexity across budgets and seeds, with almost unchanged training perplexity. The learned mixing weight also shifts upward: around step 7k, λ is about 0.056 for SLERP versus 0.030 for LERP, suggesting that the model can rely more on geometrically consistent feedback.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take about 25 minutes. Read the introduction for the failure mode of binary masking and Euclidean soft-masking. Then inspect the embedding-geometry diagnostics, especially the mask-to-prediction angle and embedding-norm plots. In the method section, check the three operations: spherical top-k aggregation, SLERP with the mask direction, and norm rescaling. In the results, prioritize MAUVE, generative perplexity, NFE-budget trends, and the learned-confidence ablation. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06398.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 层，每个动态 patch 是一个路由单元。关键设计是 router 不看 patch hidden state，只使用该 patch 的标量 entropy，通过每个 expert 一个 slope 和 bias 的仿射变换得到 expert logits。hidden state 仍作为被选中 FFN expert 的输入，因此语义信息没有从专家计算中移除，只是不参与专家选择。作者没有让高 entropy patch 激活更多专家；每个有效 patch 固定激活 2 个 expert。计算自适应来自 patcher 的粒度变化，而不是 Top-K 的动态变化。负载统计按 patch 覆盖的 byte 数加权，而不是简单按 patch 个数计数，这一点适配了变长 patch。与 Hidden-only MoE、Hidden+Entropy MoE 相比，EntropyMoE tokenizer-free in structure. BLT-style models already use next-byte entropy to form variable-length byte patches, but the global patch Transformer still applies the same dense FFN computation to every patch. EntropyMoE tests whether that same entropy signal can serve as the routing coordinate for MoE layers.","importance":"The useful contribution is not just adding MoE to a byte-level model. It clarifies how conditional computation changes when the routed unit is a variable-length byte patch rather than a fixed tokenizer token. The paper separates three roles that are often entangled: patching controls computational granularity, entropy controls expert selection, and byte coverage controls load accounting. This is a relevant design point for tokenizer-free pretraining. The evidence is still exploratory and continuation-training based, so it should not be read as a compute-matched argument against dense BLT. Its value is in showing that a low-dimensional uncertainty signal can be a stronger router than a high-dimensional hidden-state projection under this setup.","technical_approach":"EntropyMoE starts from a BLT-like byte-patch pipeline. The patcher uses next-byte entropy to create longer patches in predictable regions and shorter patches in uncertain regions. The dense FFN blocks in the global patch Transformer are replaced with Top-2 MoE layers, and each dynamic patch is routed as one unit. The central design choice is that the router only consumes the scalar entropy assigned to the patch. For each expert, a learned slope and bias map this scalar to a routing logit. The patch hidden state is deliberately excluded from expert selection, but it remains the input to the selected experts. The model therefore uses uncertainty for path selection while preserving semantic and contextual information inside expert computation. Every valid patch activates exactly two experts; the number of active experts does not grow with entropy. Byte length is used for workload accounting, which matters because patches cover different numbers of bytes.","experiments":"$e","reading_guide":"Skim in 20 to 30 minutes. Yet standard hard top-$k$ blocks gradients, while existing continuous (soft) relaxations remain too costly for large-scale models. We introduce Fast LapSum, an exact-budget soft top-$k$ primitive whose GPU solver runs in linear time after sorting. Unlike prior linear-time methods such as DFTopK, which relax the normalization const","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06912.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决大规模可微 top-k 的系统瓶颈。top-k 是 MoE routing、token/memory selection、retrieval shortlist、sparse attention 等稀疏计算里的基础操作。硬 top-k 有明确预算，但梯度几乎不可用；软松弛能传梯度，却常常需要高成本求解，或者不能严格保证选择质量之和等于 k。问题在于，稀疏化算子本身如果太慢，就会抵消稀疏模型节省下来的计算。","importance":"这不是 LLM 预训练 recipe 论文，但对稀疏架构和可训练 routing 有明确参考价值。对 MoE、稀疏注意力、可微检索、长上下文 memory selection 来说，容量预算通常是真实约束，例如专家槽位、检索条数、保留 token 数。Fast LapSum 的价值在于同时保留精确预算和端到端梯度，并把算子开销降到可以放进训练循环的水平。它更像一个底层 primitive：如果后续稀疏 LLM 需要从 hard routing 走向更稳定的可微 routing，这类算子会影响训练稳定性和系统可行性。","technical_approach":"方法基于 LapSum operator。给定分数向量 r、预算 k 和温度参数 α<0，LapSum 通过 Laplace CDF 对阈值附近的 hard top-k 决策做平滑，输出概率 p，并严格满足 ∑ p_i = k。核心难点是求阈值 b。已有实现通常用 bisection，每一步都要对全部 n 个元素重新求和，代价较高。\n\nFast LapSum 的改进是一次性计算阈值曲线。完整排序后，通过稳定的 prefix/suffix scan 得到分段曲线，再用 index lookup 和闭式 root 计算得到阈值。反向传播使用解析 vector-Jacobian product，只需要一次 reduction，不需要 straight-through estimator 或有偏 surrogate。\n\n在百万级以上，排序会成为瓶颈。为此它加入 probabilistic bracketing pipeline：用带噪 subsample 估计阈值所在区间，单次 streaming pass 只保留不确定的中间 band，再对该 band 排序，并通过 verification step 保证 bracket 正确。这样在期望意义上保持线性工作量，同时最终仍恢复精确阈值和精确预算。相比 DFTopK 通过放松 normalization constraint 换取线性复杂度，Fast LapSum 保留未放松的 ∑ p_i = k 约束。","experiments":"微基准显示，完整排序版 solver 相比逐 query bisection baseline 接近两个数量级加速。对于更大向量，bracketing fast path 在 10^7 个 scores 时比 full sorting 快 5.6 倍，并且在测试的 10^8 规模范围内保持低毫秒级开销。作者强调该方法在低于 10^6 时使用 full sort，高于该规模时用 bracket 限定排序区域。\n\n应用实验包括两个 full-scale 例子。一个是百万坐标稀疏 adversarial attack：在 ConvNeXt-V2 上，用相当于约 600 个完全饱和像素的 soft mass，在 3.3M 坐标中约占 0.02%，即可诱导误分类。另一个是 differentiable megapixel image coder，在真实 rate-distortion loop 中使用该算子。这些实验不是 LLM 任务，但说明该 primitive 能在百万级变量的端到端优化里运行，而不是只停留在小规模算子测试。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 exact budget、differentiability 和 cost routing、长上下文 token selection 或 differentiable retrieval。应用实验可快速浏览，主要用来判断系统开销量级。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles the systems bottleneck in differentiable top-k selection at large scale. Top-k is a core operation in sparse computation, including MoE routing, token and memory selection, retrieval shortlisting, and sparse attention. Hard top-k preserves an exact budget but blocks useful gradients. Soft relaxations restore gradients, but they are often expensive or fail to enforce an exact selection mass of k. The key issue is that a differentiable sparsifier cannot be more expensive than the sparse computation it is meant to enable.","importance":"This is not a base-model training recipe, but it is relevant to sparse LLM architecture research. In MoE, sparse attention, differentiable retrieval, and long-context memory selection, the budget is a real capacity constraint: expert slots, active tokens, retrieved candidates, or memory entries. Fast LapSum is useful because it preserves the exact budget while remaining differentiable and cheap enough to run inside a training loop. It should be viewed as a low-level primitive that may make softer and more stable routing mechanisms practical at the scale used by modern sparse models.","technical_approach":"$f","experiments":"Microbenchmarks show that the full-sort solver is almost two orders of magnitude faster than a per-query bisection baseline. 聚合），并指出不同度量之间不能随意拼接；早期版本正是因为把需要参考缓存的量当作运行时可观测量，被这套类型检查机制直接拒掉。","experiments":"在五个架构族、共六个模型配置上实例化了该观测框架，覆盖 dense KV、latent cache、sparse selector、recurrent state 四类内存。论文报告了各阶段本地界、跨度量桥接、以及 always-on serving/形式化，可以跳过 Lean 和完整 proof 细节；若做压缩 KV、sparse memory、hybrid RNN-Transformer 部署，值得对照自己的内存路径画一遍 update/select/read 链。预计阅读时间 25–40 分钟。","authors_note":"作者来自 metask-ai，论文附 GitHub 仓库 witprobe-attention-memory；更细的作者背景未在提供内容中明确。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Attention memory in modern serving systems is no longer a plain dense KV cache. Latent compressed caches, learned sparse selectors, and recurrent states all carry model state in different forms and fail in different ways under compression, yet the serving stack lacks a unified, composable, runtime-checkable observability framework. As a result, silent memory corruption can pass through structurally valid caches and only become visible as downstream task degradation.","importance":"The paper addresses an underappreciated reliability problem that grows as models move beyond standard dense attention: heterogeneous attention memories have fragmented failure modes, and ad hoc checks no longer suffice. Its direct relevance is strongest for long-context, compressed-cache, sparse-attention, and recurrent/hybrid serving. For pretraining researchers, the main value is indirect but real: it makes explicit the interface between architecture design and deployment-time error propagation, showing exactly how compression, selection, and reuse of internal state can turn into user-visible quality loss.","technical_approach":"Any attention memory is decomposed into three operators: update, select, and read. Each stage exposes a local contract e_out ≤ a·e_in + b with failure budget δ, with the key requirement that a, b, and δ be computable from quantities actually available at runtime rather than from an uncompressed reference. Local contracts compose by affine composition plus a union bound into a request-level risk ledger. Every number is labeled by proof strength: certified if a sound upper bound exists and is not saturated, partially certified if the bound holds only on a decidable subset, and empirical if it is an observation without a bound. Composition inherits the weakest tier, so a chain with any empirical stage cannot be reported as end-to-end certified. The framework also enforces metric typing: contracts with different error calibers, such as ℓ2 versus max aggregation, do not compose unless an explicit bridge is provided. This check caught a real bug in an early version of the authors’ own chain.","experiments":"The framework is instantiated on six model configurations across five architecture families, covering dense KV caches, latent caches, learned sparse selectors, and recurrent states. The paper reports per-stage local bounds, cross-metric bridges, and the concurrency cost of always-on probes. The authors release the measurement mathematics, acceptance gates, frozen artifacts, figure/claim generators, and a Lean development under Apache-2.0, with all numbers reproducible from a single command and build-time guards that fail on mismatch. The provided text does not include full model names or absolute accuracy/speed tables; the main empirical claim is that the framework is practical to deploy across memory classes and can catch contract-design errors in live pipelines rather than merely presenting algebraic bounds.","reading_guide":"Skim. Start with the production incident in the introduction, then focus on Section 2 for the three-operator abstraction and the certified/partially certified/empirical labeling. The key idea is not the algebra itself but the insistence that every term be runtime-computable and metric-consistent. For the local contracts, pay attention to the block-pooling and selection derivations because they show which stages attenuate or preserve upstream error versus injecting bias. Readers not working on serving or formal methods can skip the Lean details. 中的匹配方向。论文用 M_h 的 effective rank 作为 head 功能指标：低 effective-rank 表示谱集中，少数主方向主导匹配，更像 retrieval head，需要保留全局 KV；高 effective-rank 表示匹配方向分散，更接近 streaming head，可以只保留 sink tokens 和 recent window。为了部署，作者不显式构造 d_model × d_model 的 M_h，而是利用 d_head × d_head 的 proxy 计算非零奇异值。最终策略很简单：低 ER head 用 full attention，高 ER head 用 sink-plus-recent attention。整个过程不需要训练数据、校准样本或额外 gate。","experiments":"论文先给出两个观察。第一，在 Qwen2.5-7B 上，不同 head 的平均 attention distance 在 4K 到 100K context 下结构稳定，且 effective rank 与长距离 attention 行为呈负相关：低 ER head 更倾向远距离检索，高 ER head 更局部。第二，在 Llama3.1-8B-Instruct 的 32K passkey retrieval 测试中，把高 ER head 逐步改成 streaming attention 对准确率影响较小；先改低 ER head 会使检索准确率快速下降，说明低 ER head 对远程信息访问更关键。LongBench 实验覆盖三个长上下文 LLM。在 50% sparsity 下，AoH 接近 full attention，并稳定优于 random 和 reversed head selection。效率实验显示，在长上下文下它能降低 prefill/decode latency 和 KV-cache memory；文中 streaming head 使用 128 个 sink tokens 加 256 个 recent tokens，因此在 32K、64K、128K 这类长度下 KV budget 近似等于 full-attention head 的比例。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 分钟。重点看 Observations 部分的两组证据：effective rank 与 average attention distance 的关系，以及 passkey retrieval 中 low-ER-first/high-ER-first 的对照。方法部分关注 M_h = W_K^{⊤}W_Q 的定义、effective rank 的计算，以及 d_head 级别谱计算为什么等价于关注非零奇异值。实验部分看 LongBench 50% sparsity 的主表和 latency/KV memory 曲线即可。需要留意的限制是：AoH 的 head 标签是输入无关的，因此适合稳定 head 分工明显的模型；如果某些模型大量依赖动态 routing 或 layer-wise adaptive behavior，固定分类可能不够细。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether long-context attention heads can be classified without prompts, runtime attention statistics, calibration data, or learned gates. The target problem is sparse long-context inference: deciding which heads must keep global KV access and which heads can be restricted to sink-plus-recent attention from the start of prefill.","importance":"The work is primarily an inference-efficiency paper, but it is also useful for pretraining analysis. It links retrieval versus streaming behavior to spectral structure in frozen query-key weights, rather than to input-specific attention maps. This gives a weight-space diagnostic for head specialization in long-context LLMs. Such diagnostics can inform how long-range retrieval capabilities emerge during pretraining or continued long-context training, and may be useful when designing head-level sparsity or regularization schemes.","technical_approach":"Autonomy-of-Heads defines a head-specific kernel attention operator M_h = W_K^{h⊤} W_Q^h. This operator captures the query-key matching directions available to a head. The classifier uses the effective rank of M_h. A low effective rank means the spectrum is concentrated in a few dominant matching directions; these heads are treated as retrieval heads and keep full-context attention. A high effective rank means the spectrum is more diffuse; these heads are treated as streaming heads and keep only a small sink cache plus a recent window. For efficiency, the nonzero singular values are computed through a d_head by d_head proxy rather than by materializing the full d_model by d_model matrix. The resulting sparse policy is training-free, data-free, compatible with GQA-style settings, and simple enough to be applied before any prompt is processed.","experiments":"The paper first reports two supporting observations. On Qwen2.5-7B, per-head average attention distance remains structurally stable from 4K to 100K context lengths, and lower-effective-rank heads tend to attend farther back in the context. On Llama3.1-8B-Instruct at 32K context length, a passkey retrieval study shows a clear separation: converting high-ER heads to streaming attention has limited impact, while converting low-ER heads first causes retrieval accuracy to collapse quickly. The main LongBench evaluation spans three long-context LLMs. At 50% sparsity, AoH remains close to full attention and outperforms random and reversed head-selection baselines. The efficiency results show reductions in prefill and decode latency as well as KV-cache memory at long sequence lengths. Streaming heads use a small 128-token sink cache and a 256-token recent window, so at 32K and beyond the KV budget is dominated by the fraction of heads kept in full attention.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes around 20 minutes. As pruning objectives, budgets, and model architectures diversify, manually navigating the expanding design space becomes increasingly difficult. This paper aims to build an AI4AI framework for visual-token pruning by addressing a natural question: Can large language models automatically","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_07193.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 MLLM 视觉 token 剪枝策略设计过度依赖人工经验的问题。现有方法往往要手工设定 token 重要性、预算分配、约束选择和重组规则，设计空间大，而且不同 backbone、不同 token budget、不同任务下都要重新调。作者的问题设定不是再发明一个固定剪枝启发式，而是：能不能让 LLM 自动搜索并组合出有效的视觉 token 剪枝算法，同时保证生成的策略满足约束、可执行、可迁移。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接影响有限，因为它主要是推理效率工作，不是训练范式或数据方法。但它对多模态系统研究仍有两点启发。第一，它把“让 LLM 设计算法”从开放式代码生成，收缩成一个结构化、受约束的搜索问题，这种 residual search + DSL 的做法，对自动发现训练 recipe、数据过滤规则、推理控制策略都有方法论价值。第二，它强调 policy representation 比 proposer 本身更重要：不是 LLM 越强就越会设计剪枝，而是要把搜索状态表示成 LLM 能操作的形式。这个观点对很多 AI-for-AI 方向都成立。量级上，它更像一个工程方法框架，而不是改变 MLLM 基础能力边界的工作。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 AutoPrune。它不是让 LLM 从零写一个完整 pruning program，而是把搜索空间重写成“围绕强基线策略做残差修改”。这是全文最重要的设计。\n\n具体来说，作者先定义了一个 Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language，简称 TPDSL。这个 DSL 里有 131 个可复用原子，覆盖四类操作：预算控制、token 打分、选择约束、token 重组。这样做的目的，是把原本分散在代码里的设计自由度，压缩成一组结构化操作，让 LLM 不需要重新发明整个算法，只需要在已有构件上做组合和修补。\n\n更关键的是 residual state representation。每个候选策略不是一个完整新策略，而是由三部分组成：一个强 base policy、一个 TPDSL 表达的 residual modification、以及保证合法性的结构约束。运行时，系统先用 base policy 选出一批 token，再允许 residual policy 在有界 quota 内对“不确定”的选择做替换或修正。这样做有几个直接好处：一是保留人工策略里已经可靠的部分，避免 LLM 在巨大空间里盲搜；二是把搜索重点放到最影响性能的局部决策上；三是天然更容易做跨预算和跨 backbone 迁移，因为 residual 规则和具体运行时实例化是解耦的。\n\n搜索流程是 evaluator-in-the-loop。LLM 负责提出 TPDSL 残差候选，系统先做语法和约束验证，再在目标 MLLM 上评估性能和诊断指标，把反馈回灌给 LLM 继续迭代。这里的关键不是“LLM 会写代码”，而是“LLM 在一个被强约束的设计语言里做局部策略搜索”。\n\n和已有视觉 token 剪枝方法相比，AutoPrune 不主张某一种新的 importance score，而是把多种已有启发式都视为 base policy 或 DSL 原子的一部分，再通过自动搜索找到更优组合。和一般的 program synthesis 不同，它没有把候选表示成完整 Python 程序，而是显式缩小搜索空间，并把有效先验保留下来。","experiments":"实验覆盖 14 个多模态 benchmark 和 3 个 MLLM backbone。最醒目的结果有两个。第一，在 94.4% 的视觉 token reduction ratio 下，AutoPrune 在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 和 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 上都能保留超过 99% 的 full-token 性能。这个结果说明它在极高压缩率下仍能维持任务表现。第二，在 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 的 320-token efficiency setting 下，FLOPs 降低 9.9×，prefill latency 降低 6.4×，说明收益不只是理论 token 数减少，而是实际推理开销也明显下降。\n\n作者还做了几类关键验证。其一，cross-budget transfer：搜索得到的策略不需要每个预算都重新搜索，能迁移到其他 token budget。其二，cross-backbone transfer：在一个 backbone 上发现的 residual policy 能迁移到别的 MLLM backbone。其三，proposer robustness：换不同 LLM proposer，性能比较稳定，说明方法的关键在表示和搜索框架，而不完全依赖某个特定 proposer。其四，base-policy improvement：相对多个人工设计 base policy，AutoPrune 都能稳定带来增益，说明 residual search 不是只在某个基线之上偶然有效。\n\n如果你关心方法是否真的学到通用规则，最该看的是 transfer 实验和 diagnostic feedback 的消融。若去掉 residual formulation、改成从零生成完整策略，或者去掉 TPDSL 的结构约束，通常会导致搜索效率和最终性能都变差。这部分是论文最能说明设计合理性的地方。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议按“表示—搜索—迁移”三步看。\n\n先看 Introduction 和 Method 开头，理解作者为什么认为完整程序搜索不合适，以及为什么 residual search state 比直接生成 pruning code 更可行。这里是整篇工作的立足点。\n\n再看 TPDSL 的设计。重点不是记住 131 个原子，而是看它如何覆盖预算控制、打分、约束、重组四类操作，以及这些操作如何组合成合法策略。若你做过训练 recipe 搜索或系统参数搜索，这一段会比较有启发。\n\n最后看实验里的三类结果：高压缩率下的性能保持、跨预算迁移、跨 backbone 迁移。它们决定这是不是一个一次性 search trick，还是一个可复用的策略表示框架。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以完成有效泛读。若只看最核心内容，Figure 1、Figure 2 和 transfer/ablation 表格足够。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a design bottleneck in visual token pruning for MLLMs. Existing pruning methods usually depend on handcrafted heuristics for token scoring, budget allocation, constrained selection, and token reassembly, and these choices often need to be redesigned for different backbones and token budgets. Instead of proposing yet another fixed heuristic, the paper asks whether an LLM can automatically design effective pruning policies while respecting the structural constraints of the pruning problem.","importance":"Its direct relevance to pretraining is limited because this is primarily an inference-efficiency paper. That idea can transfer beyond pruning: one could imagine similar formulations for recipe search, data filtering policies, or decoding control. Existing PTQ methods typically obtain an initial quantized model through heuristic rules or greedy optimization, and once quantization is completed the resulting integer assignments are usually treated as final. This observation motivates a complementary optimization stage within PTQ that keeps quantized weights improvable after an executable quantized model has been produced, wh","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_07019.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在不改变任何部署格式（bit-width / scale / zero-point / grid layout / kernel）的前提下，如何在“已经量化完成、可直接执行”的 PTQ 模型上继续做离散优化，把先前一次性/贪心量化留下的次优整数码（integer codes）进一步改好，并且不引入反向传播或额外训练。","importance":"对大模型推理侧的直接意义更大：它把 PTQ 从“一次性生成最终整数码”改成“先得到可用解，再做固定网格上的离散精修”的两阶段流程。对预训练研究的启发主要在工程接口：1）量化不再是不可逆的终点，允许在不触碰训练数据与梯度的情况下做可控的后处理；2）对低比特（W3/W2、A4）尤其有效，能把一些激进压缩从‘不可用’拉回到‘可用’，从而影响模型发布与迭代节奏；3）方法对上游 PTQ 初始化器（GPTQ/GPTAQ/AWQ/简单 rounding 等）相对无关，适合作为统一的“最后一公里”模块。","technical_approach":"核心设定是 fixed-grid discrete refinement：输入是一个已经量化好的权重矩阵（以及其 scale/zero-point 等量化参数），这些量化参数全部冻结，只允许修改每个权重元素对应的整数码。\n\n目标函数沿用重构误差（reconstruction loss）思路，但强调推理时的 activation mismatch：层输入来自“前缀已量化”的网络而不是全精度网络，因此采用类似 GPTAQ 的 activation-aware 目标，用校准集分别跑全精度/部分量化前缀收集 X 与 \\tilde{X}，在更贴近真实推理分布的激活上做重构。\n\n优化上强调 backprop-free：不做 STE、不做梯度下降，而是把问题当作离散坐标更新（integer code 的局部搜索/迭代改进）。论文把 ReQuant 定位为可组合的 PTQ 后处理 stage：从任意 PTQ 产物出发，按 sweep 迭代地“重新审视”部分权重的离散赋值，只接受能降低总体重构损失的更新，因此保证重构损失单调不增，并在有限次接受更新后终止。\n\n从实现取舍看，它刻意冻结量化网格与推理 kernel，避免引入新的部署复杂度；代价是搜索空间受限，只能在既定网格上挪动整数码，因此收益依赖于上游网格/scale 选择是否合理。论文也强调离线预算可控：用 sweep 次数 T 控制精修强度，复杂度与一次 GPTAQ pass 同量级。","experiments":"论文宣称在 Llama-3 8B/70B、Qwen3-14B 的多种设置下（W4A16、W4A4、W3A4、W2A4）相对四个 PTQ baseline 同时改善 perplexity、KL divergence 与 zero-shot accuracy，且“初始化越简单、bit 越低收益越大”。另外给出：\n- 与 FlexRound 的 W4A16 pipeline 对比，用于说明在常见 4bit 权重量化下也能带来额外收益；\n- paired GPTQ ± ReQuant：在同一 GPTQ 初始化上加/不加 ReQuant，隔离出纯 refinement 的增益；\n- Qwen3-235B MoE：展示在超大 MoE 上可跑通，强调可扩展性。\n\n但在当前提供的正文片段中，关键数字（具体 PPL/准确率提升幅度、校准集大小、每层/每 sweep 的耗时、不同 bit 下收益曲线）尚未出现；精读时需要回到实验章节核对：收益是否主要来自少数层、是否对校准集分布敏感、以及 T 增大后的边际收益与时间成本。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：\n1）先看 Figure 1（论文在引言里提到）理解它要解决的“PTQ 把整数码当最终解”的结构性问题，以及 ReQuant 在 pipeline 中的位置。\n2）方法部分重点抓三件事：activation-aware 的目标如何构造（X 与 \\tilde{X} 的采集方式）、离散更新的单位与接受准则（如何保证 loss 下降、为何有限步终止）、以及每次 sweep 的计算复杂度如何做到接近 GPTAQ。\n3）实验部分建议按“paired study（GPTQ ± ReQuant）→ 低比特（W3/W2, A4）→ 大模型/MoE 可扩展性”的顺序读，优先确认：收益是否稳定、是否依赖特定初始化器、以及离线预算 T 的可控性。\n\n预计阅读时间：30–45 分钟可把方法与主要结论读清；若要复现，需要额外关注实现细节（校准集构造、层级处理顺序、与现有 kernel 的对齐）。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to further improve an already-deployed PTQ model by refining its discrete integer weight codes on a fixed quantization grid—without changing bit-width/scales/zero-points/kernels, and without backpropagation—so that the initial greedy/heuristic PTQ assignment is no longer treated as final.","importance":"Primarily an inference-side contribution, but it changes the practical PTQ interface for foundation models: PTQ It requires","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T08:33:13Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05747.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般的视频问答，而是 VLM 在连续第一视角视频中能否形成“全局空间表征”。更具体地说，模型是否能把沿轨迹看到的局部观察整合成一致的场景地图，并在一个从未出现在视频轨迹上的新视角下，回答“我在哪里、目标在哪里、场景整体长什么样”这三类问题。作者认为现有空间 benchmark 大多停留在单帧或少量视角，很多题目其实可以靠局部线索或单图感知完成，无法真正测到跨时间、跨视角、带全局坐标约束的空间推理能力。GST-Bench 的核心设计就是把这些捷径尽量堵住：目标物体在 query 视角中不可见，query 视角不在原视频轨迹上，答案采用距离和角度等精确数值而不是粗粒度左右前后判断，并显式引入 top-down 图像来测试局部观察到全局地图的映射能力。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它把“视频理解”里常被混在一起的局部感知、短程时序建模、全局空间整合拆开了，给出了一个更接近 embodied pretraining 需求的评测面。第二，它揭示了当前强 VLM 的失败模式并不统一：闭源大模型在把任务退化成局部可见后能大幅提升，说明主要短板在跨帧空间整合；不少开源模型即便在局部版本上也提不起来，说明感知和推理两端都弱。这种诊断对后续数据设计、架构设计、视频 tokenization 和训练目标都很有用。第三，作者不只做 benchmark，还给出同管线生成的 GST-Train，并展示定向训练能把 Qwen3-VL-8B 从 25.89 拉到 53.52，超过零样本闭源模型。这说明全局空间能力并非纯靠更大模型自然涌现，训练数据分布和任务构造本身就是关键变量。对做原生多模态预训练的人，这比单纯 leaderboard 更有信息量。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键点。其一是数据生成管线。作者基于 OmniGibson 和 BEHAVIOR-1K 的 50 个室内场景，自动采样多样化相机轨迹，并额外采样不在轨迹上的 query viewpoint。系统记录对象和视角的 3D 坐标，再投影到全局 top-down 图中，自动构造问答对。这里最重要的过滤规则是：所有 object localization 相关样本都要求目标在 query 视角不可见，从源头上避免单帧解题。其二是任务 taxonomy。围绕 embodied agent 的三个基本问题——self localization、object localization、scene structure understanding——组织成 12 个子任务。论文强调这些任务不是简单换问法，而是分别考察局部到全局映射、跨视角目标定位、全局布局恢复等不同能力。其三是诊断性对照设计。作者构造了 local variants，把目标改成在 query 视角可见，使任务更接近单图感知，从而把“看不见所以不会答”和“看得见但不会整合”区分开。这个设计很关键，因为它让 benchmark 不只是排名工具，也能定位瓶颈。\n\n从建模角度看，这篇工作本身不是提出新 VLM 架构，而是提出一种更严格的能力定义和数据构造方式。它隐含的研究判断是：如果训练目标没有迫使模型维护显式或隐式的全局空间状态，单靠通用图文视频语料，模型很容易学到局部相关性而不是可迁移的空间表征。top-down 图像的引入也很有意思，它相当于给“世界模型是否形成”提供了一个可操作的外部探针，比只问自然语言问题更接近结构化评估。","experiments":"实验部分最有信息量的是三组结果。第一组是主 benchmark 评测。作者评测了 22 个 VLM，覆盖开源、闭源以及偏 embodied 的模型。最强零样本模型 Gemini-3-Pro 只有 42.68，而人类是 79.08，差距很大。开源模型整体更低，最强的 InternVL3.5-38B 也只在 30 分左右，很多模型接近随机水平。这个结果说明当前视频 VLM 的“会看视频”并不等于“能建立全局空间地图”。第二组是 local variant 对照。闭源模型在局部版本上最高能提升 39 分，说明其单图感知和局部空间判断并不差，主要卡在跨帧整合和 novel viewpoint 推断；开源模型提升不稳定，说明问题不只是 memory 或 temporal aggregation，也包括基础视觉感知和空间 grounding。第三组是 GST-Train 微调。作者用同一生成管线构建训练集，来源包括 BEHAVIOR-1K、ArtVIP、HyperSim 和内部模拟场景。Qwen3-VL-8B 在 GST-Train 上微调后，GST-Bench 分数从 25.89 提升到 53.52，提升 27.63 分，并超过所有零样本闭源模型。这个幅度说明 benchmark 与训练数据分布高度对齐，同时也说明全局空间推理是可学的，不是单纯靠更大参数规模才能得到。\n\n从消融和分析角度，最值得注意的是作者对“局部可见/不可见”的严格控制，以及 query viewpoint 脱离原轨迹这一设置。它显著提高了任务难度，也更接近机器人探索后从新位置做判断的场景。另一个重要细节是答案采用精确数值指标，这避免了很多 benchmark 常见的模糊评分问题，使得误差分析更细。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 里对现有空间 benchmark human、闭源、开源、embodied 模型之间的差距；三是 local variant 与 GST-Train 微调结果，这两部分最能说明失败模式和可改进方向。若从预训练角度阅读，值得深挖的问题有三个：第一，现有视频预训练目标为什么没有逼出稳定的全局空间状态；第二，top-down supervision may parse local visual content from videos, yet still fail to build a globally consistent spatial representation over time. The benchmark asks whether a model can integrate egocentric observations along a trajectory, infer spatial relations from a novel viewpoint that never appears in the input video, and align local observations with an explicit top-down scene representation. This is different from standard video QA. The authors deliberately remove shortcuts: the queried target is invisible from the query view, the query camera is sampled off-trajectory, and answers are evaluated with precise numerical quantities such as distance and angle rather than coarse left/right judgments.","importance":"$13","technical_approach":"$14","experiments":"$15","reading_guide":"Start with the benchmark motivation and the three limitations the authors identify in prior spatial benchmarks. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T17:21:05Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06301.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要评测的是 AI 系统能否在固定预算内自动优化另一个 agent 的 harness。这里的 harness 包括 prompt、工具、控制流、记忆和编排代码。难点不在单次代码修改，而在昂贵、随机、反馈不完整的评测环境下，判断哪些修改真的提升了目标 agent。","importance":"这篇更适合放在 agent/eval 方向泛读。它对预训练本身没有直接方法贡献，但对 post-train 后的系统能力评测有参考价值。LLM 的实际能力越来越依赖外部 harness，单看模型权重会漏掉一部分系统层能力。论文的价值在于把“自动改进 agent 系统”从单个 demo 拆成可复现协议：固定目标模型、隔离 held-out test、限制评测预算、记录所有候选版本。这个设计能帮助区分模型能力、coding harness 能力和评测协议带来的噪声。","technical_approach":"HarnessOpt-Bench 把 harness optimization 定义成受约束的随机程序优化问题。优化器由一个 LLM 和 coding harness 组成，输入是目标 agent 的 seed harness、开发/验证反馈和有限的目标评测预算。优化器可以编辑 harness 代码库，但必须遵守固定执行接口和不可变路径约束。最终它提名一个候选 harness，在搜索期间不可访问的 test split 上打分，指标是相对 seed harness 的 normalized gain。协议强调三点：开发、验证、测试拆分固定且不重叠；held-out test 在搜索期间不可见，避免优化器拟合可见分数；trusted execution environment 负责权限隔离、预算计量、资源记录和候选版本审计。与以往自带目标 agent、自带预算、自带评测方式的 harness optimization 工作不同，这里把 optimizer model、coding harness、target agent 和 evaluation protocol 分离出来。","experiments":"实验覆盖 4 个下游任务，任务类型强调评测昂贵且随机，例如 fixed-corpus research 和 terminal use。论文在共享 coding harness 下评测 5 个 frontier optimizer models，同时也评测这些模型各自的 native harness，共得到 111 个 scored optimizer runs；另在一个任务上额外评测 2 个 coding harness。主要结果是：在 paired design 下，共享 harness 内不同 optimizer model 的差异，平均大于 shared harness 与 native harness 的差异；native harness 没有稳定优势。可获得的提升强烈依赖任务和 seed regime。OfficeQA 的 release-level 实验显示该 benchmark 能分辨连续模型版本之间的差异。轨迹分析显示，更广泛的系统级干预与更高 held-out gain 相关；细粒度 failure trace 检查使用较少，并且没有观察到与提升的正相关。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中对 expensive and stochastic evaluation 的问题定义，以及 HarnessOpt-Bench 协议部分，重点理解为什么要固定 target model/environment/verifier，并把 final test 完全隔离。实验部分可重点看 RQ1/RQ3：模型差异与 coding harness 差异的 paired comparison。Section 5.2 的 OfficeQA release-level 分析和 Section 5.3 的搜索轨迹分析值得扫一遍，它们展示了 benchmark 是否能分辨模型进步，以及当前 optimizer 的失败模式。无需细读每个任务的业务细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper evaluates whether an AI system can optimize another agent’s harness under a fixed budget. The harness includes prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code. The core difficulty is not ordinary coding, but making reliable system-level improvements when evaluation is expensive, stochastic, and only partially observed.","importance":"This is mainly a skim-worthy agent evaluation paper rather than a pretraining method paper. Its relevance is in the interface between model capability and system capability. As LLM deployments depend heavily on harnesses, evaluating only the base model or the chat model misses an important layer. The benchmark contributes a controlled protocol: fixed target model and environment, held-out final evaluation, strict budget enforcement, and full candidate auditing. This helps separate optimizer-model ability from coding-harness effects and protocol noise.","technical_approach":"HarnessOpt-Bench formulates harness optimization as constrained stochastic program optimization. An optimizer, consisting of an LLM plus a coding harness, receives a seed harness, graded development and validation feedback, and a limited target-evaluation budget. It may edit the executable harness codebase while respecting a fixed interface and immutable paths. The final submitted candidate is evaluated on an inaccessible test split, using normalized gain over the seed harness as the score. The protocol relies on fixed non-overlapping dev/validation/test splits, graded disclosure, and a trusted execution environment that enforces access control, meters target evaluations and resources, isolates held-out state, and versions all candidates. This differs from prior demonstrations where each method often used its own target agent, seed, budget, and scoring protocol.","experiments":"The suite contains 4 downstream tasks designed to have costly and noisy evaluation, including fixed-corpus research and terminal-use settings. The authors evaluate 5 frontier optimizer models under a shared coding harness and under their respective native harnesses, producing 111 scored optimizer runs. They also evaluate 2 additional coding harnesses on one task. Under this paired setup, differences among optimizer models within the shared harness are larger on average than differences between shared and native harnesses. Native harnesses do not provide a consistent advantage. Achievable gains vary strongly by task and seed regime. On OfficeQA, release-level experiments show that the benchmark can resolve variation across successive optimizer-model releases. Instrumented trajectories suggest that broader interventions correlate with higher held-out gain, while detailed failure-trace inspection is rare and not positively associated with gain.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Yet most evaluations rely on a single access modality (model APIs), perform a single run per prompt, and report accuracy as the primary outcome metric, without accounting for conditions such as web search that may have effects on model behavior in deployment. We audit these assumptions for one of the most widely-used LLMs, comparing two modalities, Chat","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T15:58:32Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06202.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.HC","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"当前主流LLM基准评测普遍依赖三个未经验证的假设：单一访问模态（API）的结果可代表实际部署行为、单轮响应足以反映模型特性、准确率可概括模型整体表现；但实际部署中用户主要通过聊天UI访问、常启用联网搜索，这些差异可能导致评测结论与真实行为脱节，尤其影响安全评估的可靠性。","importance":"指出了基准评测方法学的系统性盲区，对预训练模型的安全评测、能力对齐验证、部署前审计流程有直接指导意义——仅靠API单轮准确率不足以支撑模型安全性与部署就绪度的结论，评测协议需要覆盖访问模态、搜索条件等部署相关变量。","technical_approach":"采用2×2对照实验设计，控制两个因素：访问模态（ChatGPT聊天UI vs 对应同版本OpenAI API）、联网搜索状态（启用/禁用）。选取BBQ（偏见评测）和SafetyBench（安全评测）两个广泛使用的基准，分层抽样401条prompt，每个条件下重复采样3次，一周内完成全部采集以减少时间漂移。除传统准确率外，额外评测四个维度：响应一致性（同prompt多次输出的答案重合度）、响应文本相似度、引用来源一致性、拒答行为一致性。","experiments":"使用GPT-5.3 Instant作为被测模型，共采集4812条响应。核心结果：1）跨模态准确率差异仅2-3个百分点，但在SafetyBench上达到统计显著，且启用搜索后差异方向反转；2）启用搜索普遍降低响应一致性，模态对一致性的影响在SafetyBench上显著；3）即使答案一致，API与聊天UI常引用不同信息来源，措辞、语义相似度、是否给出推理过程均存在明显差异；4）拒答行为跨条件不一致——同一prompt在一种设置下被拒答，在另一种设置下可能正常回答。结论：仅靠单一模态、单一搜索条件、以准确率为核心的评测，会遗漏大量与安全评估相关的行为变异。","reading_guide":"泛读重点：第1章提出的三个评测假设与问题动机（可直接用于反思现有评测流程）、第2章的2×2实验设计与多维度评测指标构造、第3-4章的核心结果与对安全评估的启示。可跳过：具体prompt抽样的分层细节、部分统计显著性检验的冗余表述。预计阅读时间：30-45分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Current mainstream LLM benchmark evaluations generally rely on three unvalidated assumptions: results from a single access modality (APIs) are representative of real deployment behavior, a single response per prompt adequately captures model characteristics, and accuracy is sufficient to summarize overall model performance. In practice, however, users primarily access models via chat UIs and often enable web search; these gaps may disconnect evaluation conclusions from real-world behavior, especially undermining the reliability of safety assessments.","importance":"It identifies systematic blind spots in benchmark methodology, and provides direct guidance for safety evaluation, alignment verification, and pre-deployment audit of pretrained models: API-only single-run accuracy is insufficient to support claims about model safety or deployment readiness, and evaluation protocols need to cover deployment-relevant variables such as access modality and search condition.","technical_approach":"A 2×2 controlled study is designed with two factors: access modality (ChatGPT chat UI vs. the corresponding same-version OpenAI API) and web search status (enabled / disabled). 401 prompts are stratified-sampled from two widely used benchmarks — BBQ (bias evaluation) and SafetyBench (safety evaluation). Each prompt is run three times per condition, and all responses are collected within a one-week window to minimize temporal drift. Beyond standard accuracy, four additional dimensions are measured: response consistency (answer agreement across repeated runs of the same prompt), response text similarity, citation source consistency, and abstention / refusal consistency.","experiments":"GPT-5.3 Instant is used as the test model, with 4,812 total responses collected. Key results: 1) Cross-modality accuracy differences are only 2–3 percentage points, but are statistically significant on SafetyBench and reverse direction when search is enabled; 2) Enabling search generally reduces response consistency, and modality effects on consistency are significant on SafetyBench; 3) Even when answers agree, the API and chat UI frequently cite different information sources, and show clear differences in phrasing, semantic similarity, and whether reasoning is provided; 4) Abstention behavior is inconsistent across conditions — the same prompt refused in one setting may be answered normally in another. games，尤其是扑克这类高噪声环境中处理，并进一步考虑一个现实难点：如果评测时还想在线更新 value model 来做方差缩减，怎样避免当前对局的信息泄漏到自己的校正项里，导致统计保证失效。","importance":"它对 LLM 基座研究不是直接的预训练方法贡献，而是偏评测基础设施。启发点在于：当 agent 1.37，差异很大。论文给出的解释不是经验性的，而是由 width floor 导致：当 bounded CS 的确定性宽度下界占主导时，方差再低也换不来同量级的提前停止。\n\n在线学习部分，作者在两个受控的 profile mismatch 场景下测试 past-only value learner，结果能恢复 frozen-to-oracle variance gap 的 77% 到 79%。这说明在线更新 value model 确实能接近理想冻结 oracle 的效果，而且不需要牺牲统计合法性。\n\n论文还做了 rolling leaderboard 模拟来说明 optional stopping 的风险。在零假设下，如果持续监控普通 fixed-sample interval 并在首次有利时停下，会产生 61% 的假阳性；而用 confidence sequence 在同一停止时刻重算，几乎能推翻这些错误结论。进一步地，当 AsympCS 按自己的 stopping rule 继续运行时，到 5,000 hands 时对注入的 0.1 和 0.2 BB 效应分别能在 81% 和 100% 的条目中检测出来。最后，论文还强调可复核性：只要公开 corrected outcomes 到停止时刻、停止规则、目标、置信水平、bound 和 CS 设置，第三方就能重建早停结论。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓住三件事。第一，看 Introduction 里为什么普通 optional stopping 会失效，以及 AIVAT 与 confidence sequence 各自解决什么问题。第二，看方法部分关于 pre-action conditional-kernel interface Our controlled evaluation holds the single-shot solver fixed and varies one ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_07213.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是如何把 text-to-SQL 做得更高，而是如何衡量 test-time scaling 产生的“后续价值”。很多系统会用执行反馈、自修复、多轮采样等额外推理计算把一次失败的 SQL 修好，然后把修复轨迹存成 memory，供后续问题复用。现有评测通常只给一个端到端分数，无法区分收益到底来自重复问题的直接回放，还是来自对新问题的迁移帮助，也无法判断究竟是收集、验证、存储格式还是检索策略在起作用。作者把这个测量问题定义为 crystallization：一次性 test-time 计算，究竟有多少被“结晶”为可复用记忆，并在未来问题上兑现价值。","importance":"它和狭义预训练不直接相连，但和 pretrain/post-train 接口、test-time compute 的资产化、持续学习式 memory 设计有明显关系。更具体地说，这篇工作提供了一个很干净的评测框架，用来拆开“额外推理算力”和“可复用知识沉淀”之间的关系。对做 RL、self-improvement、online adaptation、memory-augmented agent 的研究者，这种拆解很有用，因为很多方法声称把推理经验沉淀成长期能力，但常常只报一个总分，无法判断是记住了旧题，还是学到了数据库结构、schema 习惯、join 模式这类可迁移信息。论文的贡献主要是 measurement protocol 和 attribution design，而不是新的求解器。","technical_approach":"方法核心是固定 single-shot solver，只改变 memory pipeline 的一个因素，做配对比较。作者把未来价值拆成三种使用场景。第一是 exact-query replay：同一个问题再次出现时，memory 能否直接回放已验证的正确 SQL。第二是 cross-question retention：把某个问题自己的 card 从 memory bank 中移除，再看剩余 card 是否还能帮助重新回答它，用来隔离“跨问题复用”而不是简单记忆。第三是 held-out same-database transfer：在同一数据库上留出未见问题，测 memory 对这些新问题的一次作答提升。为了把 transfer 放到可解释的尺度上，作者定义了 CR（crystallization ratio）：held-out transfer lift / 同一批 held-out 问题上的 on-demand repair headroom。这样可以回答“memory 吃掉了多少原本需要现场修复才能拿到的增益”。在具体实现上，主实验用 execution-guided repair 生成 episode，并在数据库级别建立 verified card bank。随后围绕三个研究问题做机制干预：一是价值主要出现在哪种未来使用场景；二是 transfer 到底由什么信息承载——通用示例、对齐的 question-SQL 配对，还是对目标数据库的暴露；三是收集方式、验证可靠性、card 格式、检索策略、是否必须是 repair-specific 内容，这些选择哪些真正影响结果。论文特别强调 card writer 不看 gold SQL，避免把监督信息泄漏进 memory。","experiments":"主结果基于 BIRD，并使用三组 paired seeds，同时考虑数据库和问题层面的变异。最重要的数字是：在存储 verified corrected queries 的 database-scoped bank 下，held-out first-attempt accuracy 提升 4.34 个百分点；这个提升相当于同一批 held-out 问题上 on-demand repair headroom 的 44.4%。换句话说，原本需要额外修复计算才能拿到的增益里，接近一半可以通过先前积累的 memory 在首轮作答时兑现。论文还报告 memory 在 replay 和 retention 两种设置下也有明确收益，说明它不只是“记住原题答案”，也确实跨问题传递了一些数据库相关知识。机制实验给出的结论比较鲜明：transfer 主要来自“见过目标数据库”这件事，local cards 暴露的 identifiers、values、joins、query structures 即使在 question-SQL 配对被打乱时仍然有帮助；这说明迁移载体更像数据库局部模式和查询先验，而不完全依赖精确示例对齐。另一方面，可靠验证和更广的检索覆盖能带来清晰增益；更复杂的 card 格式、更 elaborate 的 retriever、以及 repair-specific 内容本身，并没有显示出统计上可靠的额外优势。这一点很重要，因为它把很多系统工程上的复杂设计压回到了几个真正有效的因素上。","reading_guide":"如果只想抓住贡献，先看 Introduction 里对 replay、retention、transfer 三者的定义，再看 Figure 2 的 protocol，总体框架就清楚了。随后重点读实验设计部分，确认作者如何固定 solver、如何做 paired comparison、CR 的分母为什么选同题上的 repair headroom。主结果表需要细看，因为这篇工作的价值主要来自 measurement discipline，而不是模型结构。机制实验里最值得看的是“shuffle question-SQL pairing 仍保留不少 transfer”这一组，它直接支持“数据库暴露比示例对齐更关键”的结论。若时间有限，预计 25 到 35 分钟可以读完核心内容；若要深挖，可以继续看 verification、retrieval coverage、card formatting 的消融，判断哪些 memory 设计在别的任务上也可能成立。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not mainly about pushing text-to-SQL accuracy higher. It asks a cleaner question: when extra test-time computation repairs a failed query, how much of that expensive computation can be turned into reusable memory for future questions? Existing systems often store verified repair episodes, but evaluation usually collapses everything into one end-to-end score. That score cannot tell whether gains come from replaying recurring questions or from helping unseen questions over the same database, and it cannot attribute gains to specific memory choices. The paper calls this the crystallization problem: measuring how verified test-time episodes create future value.","importance":"This is adjacent to pretraining rather than directly inside it, but it matters for the pretrain-to-posttrain interface, online adaptation, memory-augmented agents, and the broader question of whether test-time compute can be converted into durable capability. The main contribution is methodological. It provides a disciplined evaluation protocol that separates one-off inference gains from reusable knowledge gains. For researchers working on self-improvement, RL-style refinement, continual adaptation, or memory systems, that separation is useful because many papers report a single aggregate improvement without showing whether the system truly transfers knowledge or merely caches solved instances.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"If you want the main idea quickly, start with the definitions of replay, retention, and transfer, then read the protocol figure. The paper is strongest where it enforces measurement discipline, so pay close attention to how the solver is held fixed, how paired comparisons are constructed, and why CR uses repair headroom on the same held-out questions as the denominator. The most informative mechanism result is the intervention where question-SQL pairings are shuffled yet substantial transfer remains; that is the clearest evidence that database exposure matters more than exact demonstration alignment. First, reference nesting creates a chain of content pages. Second, an LLM-driven decomposition pipeline splits a harmful goal into sub-steps that look individually benign while still composing into the original harmful action. The outputs fall into operational, semantic, and escalatory strategies. ar","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06940.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“验证信号有没有价值”，而是“现有面板独立性指标是否能看见这种价值”。对象是 LLM judge panel：多个模型对同一候选答案做二元判定，再用多数投票聚合。已有分析用 effective-vote count 这类总体统计量衡量面板中有多少“独立信息”，并发现不同 judge 的错误高度相关，因此多加几个 judge 收益有限。作者指出，这类总体依赖指标回答的是群体平均独立性问题，但单个外部验证信号替换一个 judge 之后，真正影响决策的样本集合是被多数投票结构严格限定的：只有原始投票差距为 1 的样本，也就是 4–3、3–2、5–4 这类 pivotal query，替换一个 ballot 才可能翻转最终判定。于是核心问题变成：验证信号的帮助是否集中在 pivotal 区域，而不是是否在总体上显著提高 effective-vote count。","importance":"对预训练本身没有直接方法贡献，但对大模型评测和 verifier 设计有一个很实用的提醒：不要把“相关性高、独立票数没涨多少”直接等同于“验证没用”。如果评测或训练闭环里使用 judge panel + verifier，例如代码、数学、可执行任务的自动评估，这篇文章给出了一种更细的分析框架：把总体依赖性和条件决策效用分开看。它的价值在于纠正评测统计口径，而不是提出新的 judge 模型。对做 post-train、RLAIF、process supervision、自动数据筛选的人，这个结论是有启发的，因为很多系统里的 verifier 本来就不是为了平均改善所有样本，而是为了在边界样本上纠错。","technical_approach":"方法分两层。第一层是一个很直接但重要的结构性结论。对奇数个 judge 的无权多数投票面板，若只允许用一个 verification ballot 替换一个 judge 的 ballot，那么最终标签会改变，当且仅当原始票差 margin m=|2s-k|=1。这个命题不依赖统计假设，也不依赖 verifier 准确率，只依赖多数投票的算术结构。作者把这件事形式化成 Proposition 1–2，并把“受影响集合”和“总体样本集合”明确分开。\n\n第二层是经验分析。作者在代码任务上构造多模型 judge panel，并引入来自另一证据源的 verification signal，考察三类替换规则：随机替换、固定位置替换、替换多数侧的一票。然后同时报告两种诊断：一是沿用 Kohli (2026) 的 n_eff/effective-vote 统计，看总体独立信息是否上升；二是按 vote margin 分层，看不同 margin 区间的错误率、替换后准确率提升、以及 verifier 调用是否可以只在 pivotal 区域触发。文章的关键点是，前者可能几乎不变，后者却能显示出稳定的条件收益。\n\n从机制上看，这篇工作不是在证明 verifier 更独立，而是在证明“独立性”不是唯一相关量。若一个 verifier 的纠错能力主要集中在 panel 最犹豫的样本上，那么它对总体相关性统计的贡献会被大量永远不会翻转的非 pivotal 样本稀释。作者还做了一个次要分析，把 pivotal 区域的错误模式与 panel 组成联系起来，说明某些组合更容易在边界样本上出现系统性偏差。","experiments":"实验围绕代码生成评测展开，覆盖 3 个 benchmark 和 4 种 panel size，k∈{3,5,7,9}。主设定里是 7 个 LLM judges 加一个 verification signal。作者先复现一个看起来矛盾的现象：在更大规模实验中，用 verification signal 替换一个 judge 后，effective-vote count 的变化约为 -0.04，95% CI 为 [-0.10, +0.02]，统计上无法区分于 0；但面板准确率仍然提升。\n\n分层后这个矛盾消失了。对至少 5/7 一致的样本，单 ballot 替换无法改变多数结果，因此准确率提升严格为 0。对 4–3 的 pivotal 样本，同样的 verification signal 带来超过 10 个百分点的准确率提升。作者还给出一个主设定中的误差分布：总共 569 个 panel error，其中 199 个发生在 pivotal 区域。也就是说，pivotal 区域不是“承载了全部错误”，但它的错误率更高，而且所有由单次替换带来的决策变化都只能发生在那里。\n\n在系统层面，作者进一步测试 margin gating：既然替换规则一旦固定，非 pivotal 样本上的 verifier 调用不会改变预测，那么可以只在 margin=1 的样本上调用 verifier。实验里这样做可把 verifier 调用频率降到 12%–27%，同时保持与“全量调用 verifier”相同的预测结果。表 7 还显示，替换规则本身会实质影响准确率；gating 只负责省调用，不负责决定哪种替换策略更优。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看 Introduction 和 The Pivotal-Vote Mechanism，弄清作者到底在反驳什么：不是反驳 panel replacement rule 是两件不同的事。\n\n如果时间有限，30 分钟左右可以读完核心内容。重点不是实验工程，而是统计口径和决策结构之间的错位。值得多想的一点是，这个结论依赖“单 ballot 替换 + 多数投票”。如果换成加权聚合、连续分数聚合、多个 verifier 联合、或 verifier 直接参与 judge 生成过程，受影响集合会怎么变，这部分文章没有展开，但很适合延伸到更一般的自动评测系统设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but important question: when a verifier helps a judge panel, why can aggregate independence metrics fail to show it? Prior work measured panel quality through effective independent votes and found that errors across LLM judges are highly correlated, so adding more judges yields limited gains. A natural extension is to replace one judge vote with a signal from a different evidence source, such as unit-test execution. At larger scale, that replacement barely changes the panel’s effective-vote count. The authors show that this does not mean verification is useless. Under single-ballot substitution in an odd-size majority panel, only one-vote-margin cases can ever change the final decision. The real empirical question is therefore conditional: are errors elevated in those pivotal cases, and does verification help there?","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for evaluation pipelines used around LLM training and post-training. If one uses judge panels plus verifiers for code, math, or executable tasks, the paper gives a cleaner diagnostic split: population-level dependence is one question, conditional decision utility is another. That distinction is useful for automated reward modeling, data filtering, RLAIF-style supervision, and verifier-assisted evaluation. A verifier may be valuable precisely because it corrects boundary cases, even if it contributes little to an aggregate independence statistic averaged over many unaffected examples.","technical_approach":"$1e","experiments":"$1f","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the pivotal-vote section. Across expert-graded research replications, legal work, and clinical-trial assessments, agreement with experts falls as the number of verdicts per call grows. 在线性空间中测量隐藏状态对真实/幻觉物体 token 的可分性。结果显示隐藏特征更可分，输出概率更含混。第二步是训练 TruthLens：选择一个很少使用的特殊 token 作为 reference token。在每个物体 token 的预测位置，读取 LM head 分配给该特殊 token 的 log-probability，并减去一个预设常数，得到 truthfulness score。训练时，对每张图采样多条 caption，标注其中物体 token 是真实还是幻觉；用 MSE 把真实 token 的分数推向 1，把幻觉 token 的分数推向 0。同时加入 divergence constraint，限制新模型偏离原始生成分布，避免检测能力提升伴随生成能力退化。推理时只读取已有 LM head 的概率，不需要额外模型，也不增加额外前向计算。","experiments":"论文在多个 LVLM 和多个物体幻觉检测基准上评估，报告相对已有 OH detection 方法有稳定提升。一个重要结果是训练标注只覆盖有限物体类别，但方法能迁移到标签空间更大的 benchmark，说明它学到的不只是类别记忆，而是某种 token-level groundedness 信号。全文片段没有给出具体数值、模型列表和各 benchmark 指标，因此定量幅度需要以实验表格为准。值得关注的消融包括：特殊 token 选择、预设常数和阈值 μ、MSE 监督、divergence constraint、不同层隐藏状态可分性，以及跨模型泛化。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 25–35 分钟。先看 Fig. 3，确认 hidden representation separability 和 LM-head ambiguity 的证据是否充分。然后看方法部分的 truthfulness score 定义，特别是为什么用特殊 token 的 log-probability，而不是直接训练一个 probe 或外部 classifier。实验部分优先看跨 LVLM、跨 benchmark、跨类别空间的泛化结果，再看 divergence constraint 是否保护原有生成质量。对预训练研究更有用的是“把隐藏层中的真实性信号接到 LM head 输出空间”这个接口设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses object hallucination detection in LVLMs without relying on an external verifier. Its central observation is that real and hallucinated object tokens are separable in hidden representations, but this separability is largely weakened after the LM head maps them into vocabulary probabilities.","importance":"The work is useful for studying the gap between internal representations and exposed model outputs. LVLM hidden states already encode some truthfulness or uncertainty signal, but standard next-token training does not force the LM head to make that signal readable. TruthLens uses a special token as a token-level truthfulness channel. This connects to verifier design, reward modeling, self-confidence calibration, and hallucination control. It also suggests that pretraining or post-training objectives can include structured readout signals instead of relying only on free-form generation.","technical_approach":"The method starts with a diagnostic comparison. Token-level negative log-likelihood is used to inspect LM-head confidence, while LDA is applied to hidden states to measure separability between real and hallucinated object tokens. The finding is that hidden states are more discriminative than output distributions. TruthLens then repurposes a rarely used special token as a reference token. At the prediction position of each object token, the method reads the log-probability assigned to this special token by the LM head and subtracts a predefined constant to obtain a truthfulness score. During training, multiple captions are sampled for each image, object tokens are labeled as real or hallucinated, and an MSE loss pushes real-token scores toward 1 and hallucinated-token scores toward 0. A divergence constraint keeps the updated model close to the original generation behavior. At inference time, the score is read from the existing LM head distribution, so no auxiliary model or extra forward pass is required.","experiments":"The paper evaluates the method across multiple LVLMs and object hallucination benchmarks, reporting consistent gains over existing detection approaches. A notable result is that the training annotations cover only a limited set of object categories, yet the learned signal transfers to benchmarks with much larger label spaces. This suggests the method is not merely memorizing category labels, but learning a token-level groundedness readout. The supplied content does not provide exact numbers, model names, or metric tables, so the magnitude of the gains should be verified in the experimental section. Important ablations include the choice of special token, the predefined constant and threshold μ, the MSE supervision, the divergence constraint, layer-wise hidden-state separability, and cross-model generalization.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes.
