A latent-source analysi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2608_07006.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个很具体、但对新型语言建模范式很关键的问题：在视觉 RAG 里，给 diffusion language model 提供更多检索证据，为什么会出现“检索覆盖更高，但最终答案更差”的反常现象。传统 AR-RAG 的直觉是 top-k 越大，答案页被召回的概率越高，生成器就更容易答对；但在多模态 DLM 中，多个页面会同时进入并行去噪过程，所有未决 token 位置共享这些视觉条件。这样一来，若不同页面分别支持不同但各自自洽的答案，模型在位置级并行提议时会把不兼容的信息拼接起来，生成一个没有任何单一页面支持的混合答案。论文把这个问题刻画为 source-coherence loss，即并行去噪破坏了“答案应来自同一潜在证据源”的耦合约束。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义不在于提出了一个通用 DLM 训练目标，而在于它把 diffusion/并行生成范式在 RAG 场景下的一个结构性弱点讲清楚了。AR 模型逐 token 条件化，天然更容易沿着单一证据链展开；DLM 的并行去噪则可能在早期就把多个来源混合。这个观察对后续几类工作都有启发：一是多模态 DLM 的上下文构造不能直接照搬 AR-RAG 的“多给证据总没错”；二是 generator-side evidence admission 需要和 DLM 的去噪几何匹配，而不是只看 retriever 分数；三是第一步去噪分布本身可以作为诊断和控制信号。这类分析对 diffusion LM、any-order LM、masked LM 一类非 AR 生成器如何接入外部知识，属于有方法论价值的补充。","technical_approach":"方法上分两部分。第一部分是机制分析。作者先在固定 top-k 输入设置下，对 3 个多模态 DLM 和 5 个视觉 QA benchmark 做系统比较，发现 Recall@k 随 k 上升，但宏平均答案准确率反而下降。随后他们把额外页面分成不同条件，指出真正造成退化的是 semantic conflict，而不是“多图输入”本身。理论上，论文在 first unresolved denoising state 上做了一个受限 latent-source 分析：若正确页和冲突页分别支持不同完整答案，而答案跨多个 token 位置，那么理想模型应在这些位置上共享同一个 latent source 选择；但位置独立的 proposal 会丢掉这种耦合，于是给 cross-source answer 分配非零概率，而且冲突跨度越长，unsupported mass 越大。第二部分是训练外的证据筛选框架 ECF。它包含 multi-granularity evidence construction 和 generator-side admission 两步。前者把整页和版面区域一起建索引，解决答案局部化问题；后者不直接依据检索分数选页，而是对每个候选页 ci，在 {c1, ci} 与 {c1, blank(ci)} 两种条件下比较目标 DLM 第一去噪步 answer block 的熵，得到 conditional confidence gain。这个设计的关键在于用 blank 对照消掉“多一个视觉输入槽位”带来的结构性影响，只测候选内容本身是否降低竞争源风险。最后再结合 rank prior 决定是否从 top-1 扩展到更多证据，以及扩展到哪一页。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个多模态 DLM、5 个视觉 QA 数据集，并在统一的 VisRAG 排序候选池、prompt、解码预算和答案评分器下比较 fixed top-k 与选择式证据接入。核心结果有三层。第一，现象层：top-k 从 1 增到 2、3 时，answer-page recall 持续上升，但在 15 个 model-dataset 组合上的宏平均答案准确率下降。第二，机制层：在作者构造或归类的页面条件中，conflicting pages 导致的精度下降最大；而且这种干扰在第一步 answer-block 分布里就能看到——在自然检索到的 ChartQA 样本上，冲突页条件下 first-step answer-block accuracy 降幅最大，与 correct-only 分布的 Jensen-Shannon divergence 也最大，并且这与最终答案退化方向一致。第三，方法层：ECF 在 5 个 benchmark、3 个 DLM 上，相比最强 fixed top-k 输入平均提升 2.62 个百分点；在 LLaDA2.0-Uni 上，相比最强 training-free baseline 平均再高 2.37 个百分点。论文还强调，ECF 的收益在 candidate pool 扩大时更稳定，说明它确实把“候选池大小”和“最终上下文大小”解耦了，而不是只在某个固定 k 上碰巧有效。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着“DLM 为什么和 AR 在 RAG 上行为不同”这个问题去读。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1、Figure 2，把 availability–accuracy mismatch 以及 first-step signal 的经验事实抓住。然后重点读 3.3 的 latent-source analysis 和对应命题，那里是全文最有信息量的部分：作者不是泛泛地说“多证据会冲突”，而是把冲突如何在并行去噪里变成 unsupported mixed answer 讲清楚了。接着看 ECF 的定义，尤其是 {c1, ci} 对 {c1, blank(ci)} 的 counterfactual 设计，理解为什么要做 geometry-matched 对照，而不是直接比较加入候选前后的熵。实验部分建议重点看三类表图：top-k 扫描、冲突类型分解、first-step 与 final-answer 的对应关系。若时间有限，读完机制分析和主结果就够；若想深挖，可以继续看 multi-granularity evidence construction 的实现细节，判断它的收益有多少来自更好的检索覆盖，有多少来自 generator-side filtering。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific but important failure mode of diffusion language models in visual RAG: retrieving more evidence improves answer-page recall, yet passing all retrieved pages to the generator often hurts final answer accuracy. The usual AR-RAG intuition is that larger top-k should help because the answer-bearing page is more likely to be included. The authors show that this intuition breaks for multimodal DLMs. During parallel denoising, multiple visual pages jointly condition all unresolved answer positions. If different pages support different coherent answers, position-wise proposals can mix incompatible sources and assign probability to hybrid answers that are supported by neither page. The paper frames this as source-coherence loss: the denoising process fails to preserve the coupling that all answer tokens should come from the same latent evidence source.","importance":"Its value for pretraining-oriented readers is not a new training objective, but a clear account of a structural weakness of non-AR generation under retrieval augmentation. AR models expand answers token by token and are less exposed to this kind of cross-source mixing. DLMs, because they denoise many positions jointly, can fail much earlier in the generation process. That matters for diffusion LM, masked LM, and any-order LM research more broadly: evidence selection cannot simply inherit AR-era heuristics such as “more retrieved context is safer.” The paper also suggests that generator-native signals from early denoising steps can be used for context control, which is a useful design direction for retrieval interfaces to non-autoregressive generators.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"Read it with one question in mind: why does retrieval behave differently for DLMs than for AR generators? Start with the introduction and the main figures showing the availability-accuracy mismatch and the first-step signal. Then spend most of the time on the latent-source analysis in Section 3.3 and the associated propositions. That is where the paper becomes more than an empirical warning; it gives a concrete mechanism for how parallel denoising creates unsupported mixed answers. After that, look closely at the ECF design, especially the counterfactual comparison between {c1, ci} and {c1, blank(ci)}. The blanked control is important because it isolates candidate content from the mere geometry of adding another visual input. In the experiments, focus on three pieces: the top-k sweep, the conflict-type breakdown, and the correspondence between first-step distributions and final answers. If reading time is limited, the mechanism section plus the main result tables are enough to capture the contribution.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇值得看机制，不是看 RAG trick：它说明 DLM 的并行去噪会放大多源冲突，先读 latent-source analysis 和 first-step 分布那部分。","en":"Read it for the mechanism, not the RAG trick: it shows parallel denoising in DLMs amplifies multi-source conflict; start with the latent-source analysis and first-step distribution evidence."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jiankun Wang","Yisen Gao","Ziwei Zhang","Xingcheng Fu","Jiaxin Bai","Chen Gao"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-language-model","visual-rag","multimodal","retrieval","source-coherence"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07006","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.07006","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.06501","title":"Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? diffusion/latent generative model 构造一个同时兼顾压缩率、可生成性和重建质量的潜空间。论文把这个问题统一到一组 KVAE tokenizer 上，覆盖图像、视频和音频三种模态。对视频和图像，核心矛盾是高压缩会降低重建细节和生成可学性，低压缩又会让后续生成模型 token 数过多、训练和推理成本过高；对音频，还要兼顾全频带、高采样率和时序连续性。作者试图给出一套偏工程化但系统的设计答案，而不是只在单一模态上做局部优化。","importance":"对预训练和统一多模态建模的意义在于：tokenizer 不是前处理，而是生成系统的一部分。它决定 latent 空间的几何结构、信息瓶颈位置、后续模型的 token budget，以及训练动态是否稳定。很多关于 AR+diffusion 联合建模、原生多模态 LM、音视频生成的讨论，最后都会落到“离散或连续表示该怎么压缩、压到什么程度、是否利于生成器学习”这些问题上。KVAE 的价值不在于提出一个全新理论，而在于把 image/video/audio tokenizer 放在同一设计框架下，明确强调 diffusability 与 compression ratio 的共同优化，并给出一系列架构和训练上的取舍。对做视觉 tokenizer、语音 tokenizer、统一 latent space 或多模态 pretrain 的人，这类工作很有参考意义。","technical_approach":"方法上，KVAE 是一组面向 latent generative model 的 encoder-decoder tokenizer。视频部分给出两个主要配置：4×8×8 压缩、16 通道的 KVAE-3D，以及 4×16×16 压缩、64 通道的 KVAE-3D。图像部分是 8 倍空间压缩、32 通道的 KVAE-2D。音频部分是 48 kHz 全频带连续 tokenizer，latent 频率 50 Hz、64 通道。论文的重点不是某一个损失函数，而是围绕“高压缩但仍适合 diffusion 学习”的整体设计。\n\n视频 tokenizer 的几个关键设计值得注意。第一，作者采用 attention-free 主干，以 Conv3D 为核心计算块，而不是在 tokenizer 内大量使用时空注意力。这样做的原因很直接：训练更省、推理时可以通过 cache 处理任意长序列，也更适合长视频和 image-to-video 等因果场景。第二，模型采用 causal setup，使用非对称时间 padding，使 tokenizer 同时适配图像和视频输入，并避免未来帧泄漏。第三，时空下采样和上采样按顺序执行，而不是一次性混合处理；这和一些视觉 tokenizer 的 patchify 方案不同，作者明确提到 KVAE-4x16x16 把 patching 留给 downsample block，而不是在最前层就做强压缩。第四，使用 spatial RMSNorm，以支持推理时 segment size 调整。第五，encoder-decoder 采用不对称参数分配，尤其在高压缩版本里显著加宽 decoder。这个设计反映出作者的判断：在高压缩 regime 下，编码器负责提取稳定 latent，解码器需要更强容量去恢复细节，因此参数不必平均分配。\n\n从全文表述看，作者还把 diffusability 当成 tokenizer 的一等公民指标，而不是只看 PSNR/LPIPS 或感知音频指标。也就是说，一个 tokenizer 即便重建不错，如果 latent 分布不利于 diffusion 学习，整体生成系统仍会吃亏。论文后半部分专门讨论 visual domain 中 diffusability 的评估，以及若干设计消融，这一点比单纯报 reconstruction 指标更接近生成系统的真实需求。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，实验覆盖 reconstruction、generation、diffusability 评估和设计消融四部分。视觉部分，KVAE-3D 与现有开源视频 tokenizer 在重建和生成两类任务上比较，指标包括 PSNR、LPIPS，以及生成侧的 Fréchet Distance 一类指标；KVAE-2D 则在图像 tokenizer 上做对应评测。音频部分使用 PESQ 等客观指标，并强调 48 kHz 全频带、50 Hz latent、64 通道这一配置。摘要声称 KVAE 在 reconstruction 和 generation 上匹配或超过开源替代方案，但当前提供的正文片段没有完整结果表，因此无法可靠复述每个数据集上的具体数值排名。\n\n即便如此，实验设计本身已经传达出几个重要信息。第一，作者没有把 tokenizer 只当作自编码器来评估，而是把它放回下游生成链路中看最终生成质量，这比单看重建误差更合理。第二，视频 tokenizer 明确针对长序列训练和因果推理场景设计，说明评测应当包含长视频或 image-to-video 相关设置，而不是只在短片段上比较。第三，论文单独讨论 diffusability 评估和设计消融，意味着作者试图回答“为什么这个 tokenizer 更适合生成”，而不只是“它重建得更清楚”。如果后续阅读全文，最值得核对的是：高压缩版本 4×16×16(64ch) 相比 4×8×8(16ch) 在生成质量、训练稳定性和 token 成本上的具体 trade-off；decoder 加宽、attention-free、顺序时空下采样这些设计各自贡献了多少。","reading_guide":"这篇文章适合按“问题定义—架构设计—生成链路验证—消融解释”四步读。先看 Introduction，抓住作者对 tokenizer 角色的界定：它不是附属模块，而是 latent generative model 的一部分。然后重点读视频 tokenizer 方法部分，尤其是 attention-free Conv3D、causal setup、spatial RMSNorm、顺序时空下采样、encoder/decoder 非对称这几处设计。这里最值得想的是作者为什么不用更重的时空注意力，以及为什么高压缩版本反而把 decoder 做得更大。\n\n接着看 reconstruction 与 generation 的主结果表，不要只盯 PSNR/LPIPS，要同时看生成指标和训练设置。再看 diffusability 评估与 ablation，这部分决定这篇工作是否只是“工程调参”，还是对 tokenizer 设计规律有更清楚的经验总结。若你的兴趣在统一多模态 pretrain，音频部分也值得看，因为它把视觉 tokenizer 的一些设计原则迁移到了高采样率连续音频。预计泛读 35–45 分钟；若要复现或用于自家生成系统，方法和消融部分需要精读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies tokenizers as a first-class component of multimodal generative systems, not as a preprocessing detail. The central question is how to build latent representations for image, video, and audio generation that jointly satisfy three competing requirements: strong compression, good reconstruction, and a latent space that is easy for downstream diffusion models to learn. In practice, these goals conflict. Aggressive compression reduces token count and makes generation cheaper, but it can damage fidelity and make the latent distribution harder to model. The KVAE family is presented as a unified answer to that design problem across modalities.","importance":"This matters for pretraining and multimodal foundation models because the tokenizer defines the effective sequence length, the information bottleneck, and part of the geometry of the learning problem seen by the generator. In latent diffusion, and even in AR-plus-diffusion hybrids, tokenizer design directly affects training speed, sample quality, and scaling behavior. The paper is useful less for a single isolated metric win and more for treating image, video, and audio tokenization under one design lens, with explicit emphasis on the trade-off between compression ratio and diffusability. For researchers working on visual tokenizers, speech/audio tokenization, unified latent spaces, or native multimodal pretraining, that framing is practically relevant.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"$24","reading_guide":"$25","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得细读 tokenizer 压缩率与生成指标的对应表；多模态预训练的成本上限常被这里决定。","en":"Read the compression-vs-generation tables carefully; for multimodal pretraining, this tokenizer choice often fixes the practical cost ceiling."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Andrey Shutkin","Denis Parkhomenko","Ivan Kirillov","Kirill Chernyshev","Kirill Malakhov","Ilia Vasiliev","Ilia Trushkin","Valeriya Kobenko","David Chikovani","Alexander Ivanov","Azat Saginbaev","Egor Silvestrov","Ivan Mikheev","Konstantin Zakharov"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["multimodal-tokenizer","VAE","audio-tokenization","video-tokenization","latent-diffusion"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05798","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.05798","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.05237","title":"In-Context Forcing: Uncovering Context Effects in Autoregressive Video Diffusion","abstract":"Current few-step autoregressive video diffusion models depend on previous fully denoised clean frames as context for all denoising steps of the current frame. However, these clean frames leak excessive local details, which causes the model to take shortcuts, resulting in compromised temporal semantics and dynamics. Inspired by the perspective of diffusion as masking, we explore the impact of noisy contexts on few-step autoregressive generation. Yet, simply applying contexts with the same noise l","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T13:54:31Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05237.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是少步数自回归视频 diffusion 中的上下文使用问题。现有方法在生成当前帧的所有去噪步骤里都使用已经完全去噪的历史帧作为条件。干净历史帧会泄漏过多局部细节，模型容易复制相邻帧纹理，而不是学习合理的时间语义和运动变化。直接把历史帧加到同等噪声水平又会削弱条件信息，导致时序一致性变差。","importance":"对文本 LLM 预训练的直接价值有限，但对 AR 与 diffusion 混合建模有参考意义。关键启发是：自回归上下文不一定越干净越好。条件信息的可见度需要和生成阶段匹配，否则会引入 shortcut 或训练-推理不一致。这个问题在统一多模态 LM、视频 token 自回归生成、masked/diffusion language modeling 中都有相似形式。","technical_approach":"方法叫 In-Context Forcing。它把 diffusion 视为一种 masking 过程，对历史上下文施加分层噪声：相邻帧保留较少细节，远距离帧保留更多信息。这样做的动机是，相邻帧最容易提供可复制的局部纹理，需要更强 masking；远距离帧更偏向提供场景和语义约束，可以更干净一些。训练侧提出 Step-wise Rolling KV Cache，在每个去噪步保存对应噪声水平的 KV cache，并在一帧去噪完成后做 bottom-up rolling update，用来维护不同噪声级别的历史上下文，同时模拟推理时的自回归状态。推理侧利用这种对完全干净历史帧依赖的解耦，引入 cross-frame causal attention，把多级 KV 的显存压力降到单一 KV cache，并支持跨帧并行去噪。","experiments":"实验覆盖标准短视频生成和长视频生成，并与 CausVid、Self Forcing、Rolling Forcing 等少步数自回归视频 diffusion 路线形成对比。论文强调的结果包括更好的时序一致性、更强的帧间动态，以及在不明显牺牲质量的情况下获得单卡推理加速。给出的全文片段没有包含具体表格数值、模型规模、数据规模或加速倍数，因此不宜补写定量结论。消融重点应是干净上下文、同噪声上下文、递减噪声上下文三者的对比，以及 Step-wise Rolling KV Cache 和 cross-frame causal attention 对训练一致性、显存、速度的影响。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。先看 Fig. 1 和 Introduction，理解 clean context 泄漏局部细节、same-noise context 条件不足、Rolling Forcing 目标分布不匹配这三个问题。方法部分重点看 In-Context Forcing 的噪声调度，以及 Step-wise Rolling KV Cache 如何维护不同噪声级别的上下文。实验部分优先看上下文噪声策略的消融、注意力可视化、长视频稳定性和推理显存/速度对比。对 LLM 研究更有用的是“上下文可见度控制”这个设计，而不是视频指标本身。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies context usage in few-step autoregressive video diffusion. Current models condition every denoising step of the current frame on fully denoised past frames. These clean frames expose too much local detail, so the model can copy nearby texture instead of modeling temporal semantics and motion. Adding the same noise level to the context removes too much guidance and hurts temporal consistency.","importance":"The work is not directly about text LLM pretraining, but it is useful for AR-diffusion hybrid modeling. The main lesson is that autoregressive context should not always be maximally clean. The amount of visible information in the context must match the generation stage; otherwise the model either learns shortcuts or suffers from train-inference mismatch. Similar issues can appear in unified multimodal LMs, autoregressive video-token generation, and masked or diffusion language modeling.","technical_approach":"In-Context Forcing treats diffusion noise as a form of masking and uses a progressive context schedule. Nearby frames are given more noise, while distant frames are given less noise. The rationale is practical: adjacent frames contain the strongest copyable local details and therefore need heavier masking, whereas distant frames mostly provide scene and semantic constraints. For training, the paper introduces a Step-wise Rolling KV Cache. It stores KV states for each denoising noise level and performs a bottom-up rolling update after a frame is completed, maintaining progressive noisy contexts while matching inference behavior. For inference, the method uses cross-frame causal attention, reducing the need for multi-level KV caches to a single cache and enabling parallel denoising across frames.","experiments":"The experiments cover both standard short-video generation and extended long-video generation, with comparisons to CausVid, Self Forcing, and Rolling Forcing-style approaches. The reported claims are improved temporal consistency, stronger inter-frame dynamics, and faster single-GPU inference without a clear quality loss. The supplied content does not include exact metric values, model sizes, data sizes, or speedup numbers, so quantitative conclusions should be checked in the tables. The most relevant ablations are clean context versus same-noise context versus progressive noisy context, plus the effects of Step-wise Rolling KV Cache and cross-frame causal attention on consistency, memory, and speed.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Start with Fig. 1 and the introduction to understand the three failure modes: clean-context detail leakage, insufficient guidance from same-noise contexts, and the objective mismatch discussed for Rolling Forcing. Then read the method sections on the noise schedule and Step-wise Rolling KV Cache. In the experiments, focus on context-noise ablations, attention visualizations, long-video stability, and memory/speed comparisons. For LLM researchers, the transferable idea is controlled context visibility, not the video benchmark itself.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"AR+diffusion混合范式的上下文设计有直接参考价值，重点看§3的渐进式噪声策略推导和实验对比图。","en":"Useful for context design in AR+diffusion hybrid modeling; focus on §3 for progressive noise strategy derivation and experimental comparison figures."},"metadata":{"institutions":["ShanghaiTech University","Tencent Youtu Lab","Zhejiang University","M. The obvious remedy, training models to resist such signals, hides a failure mode: a model that ignores all context looks robust yet is useless when the context is worth trusting. We recast the problem as selective trust and introduce MIST, a human-annotated benchmark that renders each reasoning item under four matched conditions (clean, misleading, correct-contex","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T17:59:58Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06377.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"当模型被要求“利用外部上下文/信号”来回答问题时，如何让它学会选择性信任：既能抵抗误导信号，又不会退化成一概忽略上下文，从而在“上下文确实有用”时仍能受益。","importance":"这篇更像 post-train/对齐侧的方法与评测，但对预训练研究也有直接启发：1）把“上下文鲁棒性”从单一准确率拆成可诊断的反事实条件（clean/misleading/correct/irrelevant），避免把“忽略上下文”误当成鲁棒；2）提出一个很通用的偏好优化数据挖掘与配比思路：只对那些“无上下文能答对、加误导就答错”的样本施加学习信号，并用匹配的对照条件做平衡，减少训练目标的副作用。这类‘反事实+配比’的 recipe 对持续学习、RLAIF/DPO 数据构造、以及未来把检索/工具/多模态上下文纳入训练目标时都可复用。","technical_approach":"核心由两部分组成。\n(1) MIST-1000 基准：每个题目固定 question/answer space/gold answer/一个“看起来合理的错误答案”，只改变包裹在问题外的上下文，构造四个版本：clean（无额外上下文）、misleading（上下文指向错误答案）、correct-context（上下文支持正确答案）、irrelevant（相关但不提供答案的自然文本）。这样四个条件之间的差异主要归因于“上下文信号”而不是题目难度变化。数据来源为 1000 题：800 来自既有 QA/数学/推理基准改写，200 为标注者撰写的更长更难场景；两轮人工流程控制歧义、泄漏、自然性与错误答案的可置信度。\n(2) 评测指标与训练方法：\n- SC2W（signal-induced correct-to-wrong）：只在模型 clean 条件下答对的子集上，统计 misleading 条件把答案从对翻到错的比例，用来把“模型本来就不会”与“被信号带偏”分离。\n- SCOPE（Selective Context Preference Optimization）：一个信号反事实的偏好优化框架。先用 base model 挖掘关键失败样本：clean 能答对，但 misleading 会答错。对每个样本构造偏好对：truth-consistent response（与 gold 一致）优于 signal-following response（跟随误导信号）。关键不是改 DPO 公式，而是做‘配比平衡’：同一对偏好样本在四种上下文条件下复用，并对 misleading 对与 clean/correct/irrelevant 对照条件等权加权，避免只在 misleading 上训练导致模型学到“默认不信上下文”。这相当于把学习信号绑定到“上下文的角色（误导/支持/无关）”而不是某个表面 prompt。","experiments":"全文信息显示的关键实验结论包括：\n- 基准观察：从闭源前沿模型（文中点名 GPT-5.5、Claude、Gemini）到多种开源权重模型，普遍存在“误导上下文把原本正确答案翻错”的现象；单看 misleading 条件准确率会掩盖问题，因为强模型 clean 本来就高。\n- 方法对比：prompt 防御、SFT、以及只用 misleading 对做 DPO，往往能抬高 misleading 条件表现，但会牺牲 clean 或 correct-context（典型副作用是学到‘不信上下文’）。\n- SCOPE 效果：在常见开源模型上显著降低 SC2W，同时不侵蚀 clean / correct-context / irrelevant 三个对照条件；并且在未在目标数据集上训练的情况下，能 zero-shot 迁移到 3 个外部基准，竞争方法通常以牺牲准确率换鲁棒。\n（你给的全文片段未包含具体表格数字、训练步数、模型参数与提升幅度；这些通常在实验章节的主表与消融表中，需要结合原文表格补齐。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看 Introduction 里对“选择性信任”三目标的拆解（拒绝误导、利用正确信号、对无关信号不动），这是全文动机与评测设计的核心。\n- 精看 MIST benchmark 构造细节：四条件如何保证 only-context-changes、错误答案如何保证 plausibility、以及两轮标注/审校如何控制泄漏与歧义；这决定了指标是否可信。\n- 看 SC2W 的定义与报告方式：它把 susceptibility 从 base ability 中剥离出来，是这篇最可复用的评测思路。\n- 看 SCOPE 的数据挖掘与配比：重点是“只挖 clean-correct→misleading-wrong 的反事实失败”以及“与三种对照条件等权平衡”。如果要复现/迁移到 RAG 或工具调用场景，这部分是 recipe。\n- 最后扫一遍外部基准 zero-shot 迁移实验，判断它学到的是‘任务特定防御’还是更一般的上下文信任策略。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to train and evaluate LMs to *selectively* trust external context: resist misleading cues without collapsing into a degenerate strategy that ignores all context, while still benefiting from genuinely helpful context and staying invariant to irrelevant context.","importance":"Primarily a post-training / alignment paper (preference optimization + benchmark), but it carries concrete lessons for foundation-model training: (1) it turns “context robustness” into a counterfactual, multi-condition diagnostic (clean/misleading/correct/irrelevant), preventing the common failure where “ignoring context” looks robust; (2) it proposes a reusable data-mining + mixture-balancing recipe for preference training—optimize only on counterfactual flips (clean-correct → misleading-wrong) and balance them with matched control conditions to avoid over-regularizing the model into blanket distrust. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-27T15:22:55Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05168.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"强模型在推理题上经常不是完全不会，而是在中间轨迹进入了可修复的错误分支。论文研究一个更细的问题：弱模型生成的短局部 mid-training 或 verifier-guided pretraining，而不是关注具体数学题成绩。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a local failure mode in reasoning: a strong model may have enough capability to solve a problem, but its intermediate trajectory enters a recoverable wrong branch. The question is whether a weaker model can expose and repair such local reasoning bugs, and how to train the strong model to internalize the repair signal rather than relying on test-time patch insertion.","importance":"The contribution is mainly in reasoning post-training and weak-to-strong supervision, not in base pretraining. Its useful signal for pretraining researchers is the training target: supervision can be derived from how a local intervention changes the model’s future distribution, instead of from complete solutions or surface-form chain-of-thought imitation. This is relevant to process supervision, reasoning data generation, and the interface between pretraining, mid-training, and reasoning-oriented fine-tuning.","technical_approach":"Woodpecker Distillation treats the weak model as a diagnostic probe rather than a teacher that provides full solutions. Given a problem x and an intermediate prefix c produced by a strong model A, a weak probe model B samples several short reasoning patches. Each patch is inserted after the same prefix, and A continues generation. Patches are labeled as positive or negative according to whether the final continuation reaches the correct answer. The method keeps a trainable strong model A_theta and a frozen reference model A_ref. It uses A_ref to estimate the future token distributions induced by positive and negative interventions, then builds a contrastive teacher distribution that emphasizes continuations associated with successful repair. A_theta is trained to match this teacher. This differs from patch imitation, which only copies weak-model text, and from full-trajectory imitation, which can dilute the local correction signal over many downstream tokens.","experiments":"The paper instantiates the strong model as Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 and the weak probe as Gemma-3-4B-IT. Evaluation covers AIME 2024, AIME 2025, MATH-500, Olympiad, and Omni-Hard, with Omni-Hard containing high-difficulty Omni-MATH problems. The initial analysis focuses on problems where the strong model fails under greedy decoding, then compares direct continuation from the same prefix against continuation after inserting a weak-model patch. Table 1 reports Pass@16 improvements from the A-B-A intervention, with larger gains on harder sets such as AIME 2025 and Omni-Hard. The important ablation is that directly fine-tuning on weak patches or full repaired trajectories is less reliable than distilling the contrast between successful and unsuccessful intervention-induced future distributions.","reading_guide":"Skim in 25–35 minutes. To bridge this gap, we propose a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T17:24:36Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06310.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是生成式 reward model 在 LLM 强化学习中用不好的问题。生成式 reward model 擅长比较两个或多个回答并给出文字化判断，但现有 RL 算法通常需要每个回答一个标量 reward。把生成式模型强行转成 token probability 或无推理的偏好 token，会丢掉它在比较、解释和 test-time compute 上的优势。","importance":"这篇与 post-train/RL 训练信号质量直接相关，适合泛读。它的主要启发是：reward 不一定要由 reward model 直接输出标量，也可以先让模型做它更擅长的排序，再把排序结构转换成 RL 可用的标量。这个思路对 RLHF/RLAIF、reasoning RL 和 generative verifier 都有参考意义。尤其是推理型任务中，生成式 judge 的 CoT prompt 下当前 policy 采样出的多个回答进行相互比较，得到相对质量顺序，再把排名映射为 reward。这样 reward 来自同批样本内部的竞争关系，适合 GRPO 这类组内相对优化方法。第二是 anchor-guided ranking：引入少量 anchor responses 作为参考点，让每个候选回答与 anchor 比较，降低大规模样本排序时的调用成本。论文给出的复杂度从约 O(m log m) 的排序式比较转为 O(m n)，其中 m 是候选回答数，n 是 anchor 数；当 n 很小时，扩展性更好。构造出的 reward 可以接入 GRPO 和 DAPO 等现有 RL 算法。与 GenRM 使用指定 preference token 概率不同，RRC 不依赖单个 token 概率；与 GRAM 去掉显式 CoT 不同，RRC 允许生成式 reward model translated well into LLM reinforcement learning. These models are naturally good at comparative judgment: given multiple responses, they can decide which is better and explain why. Standard RL pipelines, however, expect one scalar reward per response. Converting a generative judge into a scalar through preference-token probability, or removing explicit reasoning to emit only a label, underuses the model’s comparative and test-time reasoning strengths.","importance":"This is a useful skim for post-training and RL signal design. The main idea is that a reward model does not have to emit scalar rewards directly. It can first perform the operation it is better suited for—ranking responses—and the ranking can then be converted into scalar rewards for policy optimization. This is relevant to RLHF, RLAIF, reasoning RL, and generative verifiers. It gives a practical interface between generative judges with chain-of-thought style evaluation and existing algorithms such as GRPO or DAPO.","technical_approach":"$28","experiments":"The experiments cover 6 benchmarks spanning open-ended chat and reasoning tasks. verified synthesis paradigm enables scalable agent training data generation without human intervention, offers direct reference for constructing SFT/RL datasets for long-horizon terminal agents, and demonstrates a solution-first approach for building general verifiable synthetic data pipelines.","technical_approach":"RST (Recursive Synthetic Terminal Tasks) is a recursive verified task synthesis framework. Starting from verified seed tasks, it first extends the reference solution to create a longer and more complex workflow, then realigns the verifier and instruction to match the new solution, and finally validates the complete task in an isolated sandbox where the reference solution must pass the private verifier. Accepted tasks are used both to collect training trajectories and as seeds for the next synthesis round. Built on a solution-first design, every accepted task carries an executable proof of solvability.","experiments":"Using DeepSeek-V4-Pro as the generator, 15 rounds of recursive synthesis produce 37,484 verified terminal tasks from 639 seeds, at approximately $0.05 per passed task with no human involvement. Task difficulty grows substantially across rounds: median solution length increases 5.6×, command usage 6.1×, while instruction length only grows 1.4×. Pass rates of identical solvers drop sharply from seed round to round 15: 72% to 4% for DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and 72.2% to 7% for GPT-5.6-sol. SFT with Qwen3.5 self-collected trajectories improves Qwen3.5-27B and Qwen3.5-122B-A10B by up to 10 points on three terminal benchmarks. PPO training further boosts Qwen3.5-27B by 8.24%, 9.33%, and 3.97% on the three benchmarks respectively. 格点，做成对奖励差分析，找出包含意义下最小的修复，而不是用“加一整束检测器后攻击变少”这种粗结论。这里 L 是“引用了未被检索到的真实语料段落”的精确 membership 检查。","experiments":"在 HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue 上用四个 Qwen3-8B 轨迹池做审计，共分析 3,829 条隔离轨迹。基础奖励 R0 已经能拒绝 same-final 搜索删除和假 ID，但无法挡住无标注的 adaptive citation laundering：攻击者把引用替换成语料中真实存在但未检索的段落，依然能拿高分。2^3 消融显示，单独增强 L 项就是观测到的最小修复；其余两项精确检查在这些数据池上是冗余的。加入 L 后，R[L] 在报告的经验攻击上 ASR 为 0，额外代价约为 0.50% Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD stil","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T16:29:24Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06243.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"RLVR使用稀疏的序列级奖励，难以做时间信用分配。On-policy self-distillation（OPSD）通过特权教师提供稠密token级分布监督来缓解信号稀疏，但vanilla OPSD对所有位置的局部KL发散赋予均匀权重，忽略了rollout中发散值的时序结构——相同的局部分散值可能出现在完全不同的发散历史之后，均匀加权无法区分这些时序上下文。","importance":"指出了vanilla OPSD中一个具体且重要的缺陷：均匀系数分配使得目标函数对发散值的时序排列不敏感。提出的DASH方法通过路径依赖的门控递归实现自适应时序加权，用可忽略的额外开销在数学推理基准上取得一致提升。对预训练研究的启发在于：稠密监督信号的时序加权结构值得更细致的设计，这与预训练中不同位置loss权重分配、RL预训练中的信用分配问题有共通的结构。","technical_approach":"DASH的核心是divergence-adaptive propagation gate：在每个位置t，计算局部分散值与序列级平均分散值的差距，将其转化为自适应传播门λ_t。这些门控控制一个反向多步递归——从后往前传播，每个局部损失的有效系数由其前面所有位置的门控路径共同决定，从而实现路径依赖的动态权重分配。这一设计的动机来自对on-policy目标精确梯度的分析：精确梯度包含一个轨迹项，其系数依赖于后续位置的发散值，呈现顺序依赖结构，而vanilla OPSD只保留了直接蒸馏项，丢弃了这一时序结构。","experiments":"在三个数学推理基准、三个模型规模上，DASH取得了对比方法中最高的综合分数，额外训练开销可忽略。消融实验验证了：(1)基于局部-均值发散差的门控比固定门控更有效；(2)多步反向聚合优于单步加权；(3)自适应监督horizon在不同长度的推理链上均有收益。","reading_guide":"重点关注Section 4.1对vanilla OPSD时序系数分配缺陷的形式化分析（均匀加权使得目标对发散排列不变），以及Section 4.2中DASH门控递归的具体构造。从exact on-policy gradient推导结构参考的部分是理解方法动机的关键，值得细读。预计阅读时间20分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"RLVR relies on sparse sequence-level rewards, making temporal credit assignment difficult. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense token-level distributional supervision via a privileged teacher, but vanilla OPSD applies uniform weights to all local KL divergences regardless of temporal context. The same local divergence magnitude can follow entirely different discrepancy histories along a rollout, and uniform weighting cannot distinguish these temporal contexts.","importance":"Identifies a concrete and significant limitation in vanilla OPSD: uniform coefficient allocation makes the objective invariant to permutations of divergence values along the sequence, discarding temporal structure. DASH addresses this with path-dependent gated recursion for adaptive temporal weighting, yielding consistent gains on math reasoning benchmarks with negligible overhead. The broader lesson—that dense supervision signals require carefully designed temporal weighting—connects to loss-weighting questions in pretraining and credit assignment in RL pretraining.","technical_approach":"DASH introduces divergence-adaptive propagation gates: at each position t, the gap between the local divergence and the sequence-level mean divergence is converted into an adaptive gate λ_t. These gates control a backward multi-step recursion, propagating from the end of the sequence backward so that each local loss's effective coefficient is determined by the full path of preceding gates. This design is motivated by analyzing the exact gradient of the expected on-policy objective, which contains a trajectory term with coefficients that depend on subsequent divergences—an order-dependent structure that vanilla OPSD discards by retaining only the direct distillation term.","experiments":"Achieves the highest overall scores among compared methods across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model scales, with negligible additional training cost. Ablations validate that: (1) gates based on local-mean divergence gaps outperform fixed gates; (2) multi-step backward aggregation outperforms single-step weighting; (3) adaptive supervision horizons provide gains across reasoning chains of varying lengths.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 4.1 for the formal analysis of the temporal coefficient allocation gap in vanilla OPSD (uniform weighting makes the objective permutation-invariant to divergence values), and Section 4.2 for the concrete construction of the DASH gating recursion. The gradient analysis deriving structural guidance from the exact on-policy gradient is the key to understanding the method's motivation and deserves careful reading. We study OPD and its advanced variant, On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$), for mathematical reasoning in English, Korean, and Japanese. OPD$^2$ improves OPD by using the probability gap between a post-trained teacher and its base model as the learning signal. Experiments with Qwen3 show that OPD$^2$ consiste","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T09:37:49Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05802.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是：on-policy distillation 这类替代 RL 的后训练方法，在多语言数学推理里是否仍然有效，尤其是对韩语、日语这类非英语语言，token 级蒸馏信号到底是在提升目标语言推理，还是只是把英语推理能力迁移并外溢到别的语言表面上。论文进一步聚焦 OPD2 的 delta signal 设计，即不用 teacher-student 的概率差，而用 post-trained teacher 相对其 base model 的概率增量作为监督，试图把“后训练新增的推理能力”从 teacher 原本已有的通用语言分布里剥离出来。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在于提出新的 pretrain objective，而在于它把 pretrain 与 post-train 的接口问题讲得更具体：如果 teacher 的能力增量可以通过 delta signal 被更干净地提取，那么后训练得到的推理能力就更像一种可迁移的“残差信号”，而不是把 teacher 的全部分布偏好一并蒸馏过去。这个视角对 reasoning-oriented continual pretraining、mid-training、以及用强 teacher 反哺 base model 都有启发。另一个重要点是，论文提醒多语言增益不能只看 benchmark 分数；英语-only 蒸馏能抬高韩语/日语成绩，但可能伴随 reasoning trace 英文化，这说明语言能力迁移和目标语言建模质量不是一回事。","technical_approach":"方法上延续 OPD / OPD2 框架。OPD 的基本做法是：学生模型先按当前策略生成 rollout，再把这条 student trajectory 喂给 teacher，在每个已采样 token 上取 teacher 的条件概率，对学生做 token-level KL 或等价的 token reward 优化。这样做和 sequence-level RL 不同，监督更细，且仍保持 on-policy。OPD2 的改动很小但针对性很强：奖励不再是 log p_teacher(y_t|x,y_<t) - log p_student(y_t|x,y_<t)，而是 log p_teacher - log p_teacher_base。也就是把 teacher 相对其 base 的增量当作监督信号。这样做的动机是减少 teacher 中那些 base 已经具备的通用语言偏好、风格偏好、格式偏好对蒸馏的干扰，把学习重点放在 post-training 新获得的 reasoning preference 上。论文还沿用了 OPD2 原始工作里的两个稳定化部件：一是 reward centering，把 reward 转成 advantage 以缓解收敛点问题；二是用原始 OPD 信号做 conditioning criterion，避免 delta signal 单独使用时训练目标过于脆弱。实验设置上，作者构造了英/韩/日三语数学训练集，总规模 100K，考察多种语言配比与 EN-only 训练情形，核心比较对象是 OPD 与 OPD2 在 Qwen3 系列 instruction-tuned 模型上的表现差异。","experiments":"从正文可确认的关键信息有三点。第一，训练数据是一个 100K 规模的多语言数学数据集，覆盖 English、Korean、Japanese，用来做 on-policy post-training。第二，结论上 OPD2 在多语言设置里稳定优于原始 OPD，且在韩语、日语上的提升更明显，说明 delta signal 在非英语推理迁移里更有用。第三，OPD2 往往能缩小 English 与 Korean 的性能差距，但这种 gap closing 不是所有模型和设置都一致成立，作者对此保持了克制。另一个实验观察很重要：只用英语数据做 OPD，也能提高韩语和日语 benchmark 分数，但生成分析显示，模型常把中间推理过程部分切换到英语，说明分数提升里混入了跨语言推理 trace 泄漏，而不完全是目标语言 reasoning 真正增强。由于给出的全文片段没有包含完整表格和具体数值，无法可靠复述每个模型、每个 benchmark 的精确提升幅度；但论文的主要实验价值不在绝对分数，而在于把 multilingual OPD 的收益与语言漂移副作用同时揭示出来。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，重点抓三个研究问题：OPD2 是否仍优于 OPD、是否缩小语言差距、EN-only OPD 是否真的提升目标语言推理。然后看 On-Policy Distillation 一节，弄清 OPD 与 OPD2 的 reward 定义差别，这是一切实验现象的解释起点。实验部分建议优先看主表和作者对生成样例的分析，尤其是非英语输入下 reasoning trace 是否混入英语。这个现象比单纯分数更值得看。预计 15 到 25 分钟可以抓住主要信息；如果只关心方法，不必深究附录里的全部 benchmark 细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether on-policy distillation remains effective for multilingual mathematical reasoning, especially in Korean and Japanese, and whether the gains reflect genuine target-language reasoning improvement or merely transfer of English-centric reasoning behavior. It further studies OPD2, where the supervision signal is the probability gap between a post-trained teacher and its base model, rather than the gap between teacher and student.","importance":"Its value for pretraining researchers is indirect but real. The work sharpens the pretrain–post-train interface: if the useful part of post-training can be isolated as a delta over the base model, then reasoning capability may be distilled as a cleaner residual signal instead of copying the teacher’s full distribution. That matters for continual pretraining, mid-training, and teacher-assisted capability transfer. The multilingual findings are also important: benchmark gains alone can be misleading, because English-only distillation may improve Korean/Japanese scores while shifting the reasoning trace toward English.","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"Three findings are clear from the full text provided. First, the training corpus contains 100K multilingual math problems spanning English, Korean, and Japanese. Second, OPD2 consistently outperforms vanilla OPD in multilingual settings, with stronger gains in Korean and Japanese than in English. Third, OPD2 often narrows the English–Korean gap, though the effect is not universal across all models and settings. A particularly useful observation is that English-only OPD can still raise Korean and Japanese benchmark scores, but qualitative inspection shows that the model often drifts into English during the reasoning process. That means some of the measured gain comes from cross-lingual reasoning-trace leakage rather than improved target-language reasoning per se. The excerpt does not include the full result tables, so precise benchmark-by-benchmark numbers cannot be stated reliably here.","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and focus on the three research questions: whether OPD2 still beats OPD, whether it reduces language gaps, and what English-only OPD is actually doing. In the experiments, prioritize the main result tables and any qualitative examples showing language drift in the chain of thought. That analysis is more informative than the headline scores. Current state-of-the-art approaches primarily rely on iterative, training-time unlearning via fine-tuning. However, even when utilizing parameter-efficient dimensionality reduction techniques like LoRA, gradient-based optimization remains computationally expensive and lacks explicit analytical formulations. It can also leave the targeted knowledge merely hidden","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T09:16:53Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05783.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 LLM 机器遗忘里一个很具体但长期存在的问题：现有主流方法大多依赖梯度微调，在 forget set 上压低概率、在 retain set 上尽量保性能，但这种做法成本高、调参重，而且遗忘往往不够“物理”。论文指出，很多微调式遗忘只是把目标知识藏进了小幅权重扰动里，低比特量化后会被重新暴露。作者要解决的不是再设计一个更稳的遗忘 loss，而是把遗忘直接改写成一个可解析求解的线性代数问题：对选定层做一次加性更新，在不破坏保留数据输出的前提下，显式压制需要删除的 token 或表征方向，并且整个过程不做反向传播、不做迭代优化。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在于“部署一个更快的删数据工具”，而在于它把参数编辑、遗忘、局部再训练这几件事拉回到一个更可分析的框架里。第一，GROM 给出了 closed-form 的 layer edit 视角：遗忘不一定要靠 SGD 近似求解，也可以在局部线性化假设下直接解最优更新。第二，它把 retain/forget trade-off 写成了带 ridge 正则的约束最小二乘问题，这对理解“为什么某些遗忘方法会伤 utility、为什么量化会恢复知识”很有帮助。第三，论文强调了量化恢复攻击这一检验维度，提醒人们不要只看遗忘后表面指标，而要看参数更新是否真的把目标映射移出了模型可恢复的表示子空间。量级上，这更像一种有分析价值的方法论文，而不是会直接改写大规模预训练流程的范式工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心是对单个权重矩阵 W 做解析更新。被编辑的矩阵可以是 LM head，也可以是 MLP block 的 down-projection。作者先对 forget 数据和 retain 数据各做一次前向传播，收集进入该矩阵的输入向量，按列堆叠成 key 矩阵；这些 key 只在“下一个 token 应该被遗忘或保留”的位置提取，因此编辑目标是 token-level 的，而不是整段样本级别的。\n\n随后，作者构造一个 ridge-regularized least-squares 目标。直观上有两部分：一部分要求编辑后的矩阵在 forget keys 上把输出推向预设的“遗忘目标”；另一部分要求在 retain keys 上尽量保持原输出不变。由于目标对 ΔW 是二次凸的，最优加性更新 ΔW* 可以闭式求出。这样做和 LoRA/GA/RKLD/NPO 这类方法的差别很明确：它们通过梯度下降在参数空间里逐步逼近一个折中点，GROM 则直接解出该折中点。\n\n遗忘目标的设计有两类。其一是 token suppression：直接压低目标答案 token 的 logit 或相关输出，使模型不再偏向原本记住的内容。其二是 representation corruption：不是只盯着最终 token，而是把某些中间表征推离原方向，让后续层更难恢复被删信息。论文还配了 logit-lens layer selection，用中间层经 LM head 投影后的可读性来判断哪些层已经显式承载了待删知识，从而只编辑少数高影响层，避免全模型改动。另一个有意思的部分是 influence auditing：因为解是解析的，作者可以精确分解单个删除请求对最终更新的贡献，而不是像梯度法那样只能做近似归因。这一点对合规审计和批量删除请求冲突分析都很实用。","experiments":"论文在五个 benchmark 上比较了遗忘-保留权衡，并把 gold retrain 作为上界参照。主结论有三点。第一，效率上，GROM 只需要每层一对 forget/retain 前向统计和一次闭式求解，运行时间比梯度式 baseline 快到两个数量级，作者把完整遗忘过程描述为“秒级”。第二，效果上，在 Pareto 前沿上通常优于或接近 GA、GradDiff、RMU、RKLD、NPO、SimNPO 等方法，也就是在相同 utility 损失下忘得更多，或者在相同 forgetting 强度下保留更多原能力。第三，鲁棒性上，针对近期提出的低比特量化恢复攻击，梯度式强 baseline 在量化后会恢复相当一部分本应删除的内容，而 GROM 的遗忘程度基本维持，接近 retrain 模型。\n\n从文中方法细节可以看出，实验真正支撑结论的不是单一平均分，而是几类消融：编辑哪一层、编辑 LM head 还是 MLP down-proj、token suppression 与 representation corruption 的差异、ridge 系数大小、retain 集规模，以及多请求叠加时的相互干扰。论文的隐含信息是：如果层选得准、retain 约束足够强，局部线性编辑可以覆盖相当一部分“记忆型”遗忘需求；但若目标知识是分布式、跨层冗余编码的，单层或少层编辑的上限仍然存在。当前给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字，因此无法逐项复述具体 benchmark 分数，但速度量级、Pareto 结论和量化鲁棒性是文中最关键的实验信息。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 里对“量化后遗忘失效”的动机，这决定了为什么作者坚持做 closed-form 而不是再调一个更强的遗忘 loss。然后直接看 Method 里目标函数和闭式解推导，重点理解三件事：forget target 是怎么定义的，retain constraint 是怎么进入矩阵求解的，ridge 正则在数值稳定性和保留性能之间起什么作用。接着看 layer selection 部分，判断作者为什么选 logit-lens 而不是凭经验固定编辑后几层。实验部分建议优先看三类图表：forget/utility Pareto 曲线、运行时间对比、量化攻击前后遗忘指标变化。预计 30–45 分钟可以抓住主线；如果要深挖，值得推一遍闭式解的矩阵形式，并思考它和 ROME/MEMIT 一类知识编辑方法在约束形式上的关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific weakness in LLM unlearning: most strong methods rely on iterative gradient-based fine-tuning to reduce likelihood on a forget set while preserving utility on a retain set, but this is expensive, tuning-heavy, and often only hides the unwanted knowledge. The authors argue that such forgetting can be fragile: low-bit quantization may recover content that appeared erased. Their goal is not to design yet another unlearning loss, but to reformulate unlearning as an analytically solvable matrix update problem. The model applies a one-shot additive edit to selected layers, suppresses targeted outputs or representations, preserves retained behavior, and does so without backpropagation or iterative optimization.","importance":"Its main value for foundation-model research is conceptual rather than purely operational. GROM shows that unlearning, local editing, and constrained post-hoc model updates can be cast in a closed-form optimization framework instead of being treated as another fine-tuning recipe. That matters for three reasons. First, it gives a more interpretable view of the retain-forget trade-off through ridge-regularized least squares. Second, it offers a concrete explanation for why some gradient-based unlearning methods may fail under quantization: the unwanted behavior may survive in a recoverable low-magnitude subspace. Third, it suggests that at least part of model memorization can be manipulated through targeted linear edits, which is relevant to broader questions around memorization, parameter locality, and post-pretraining control. This is better read as an analytically useful method paper than as a change to large-scale pretraining practice itself.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the motivation around quantization-induced recovery of forgotten content. That explains why the paper insists on a closed-form update instead of another stronger fine-tuning objective. Then go straight to the optimization setup and derive the closed-form solution at a high level: how the forgetting targets are defined, how retain constraints enter the solve, and how ridge regularization trades off stability against preservation. After that, read the layer-selection section to understand why the authors use logit-lens diagnostics rather than editing a fixed set of late layers. In experiments, prioritize three plots or tables: forgetting-utility Pareto curves, runtime comparisons, and before/after quantization forgetting metrics. Around 30 to 45 minutes should be enough to capture the main idea. If reading more deeply, compare the constraint structure to knowledge-editing methods such as ROME or MEMIT.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"揭露梯度类机器遗忘的核心缺陷：仅隐藏知识而非真删除，量化即可复原，闭式解思路简洁可复用","en":"Exposes a critical flaw in gradient-based unlearning: knowledge is merely hidden not removed and recovers after quantization; the closed-form solution is simple and reusable."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Heinrich Heine University Düsseldorf","Jagiellonian University","IDEAS Research Institute"],"authors":["Paweł Batorski","Przemysław Spurek","Paul Swoboda"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["machine_unlearning","privacy","gradient_free","closed_form_update"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05783","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.05783","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.06352","title":"CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks","abstract":"Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T17:53:18Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06352.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的不是“如何生成可执行 terminal 任务”，而是更难的一层：如何把任务构造成对某一类求解器来说“可学但不平庸”的训练样本。仅靠 executable validation 只能证明任务能跑、能验收，不能判断它对当前 agent 是太简单、太难，还是刚好处在能提供学习信号的区间。CalibForge 把这个问题形式化为 solver-relative learnable zone：任务必须被至少一个求解器真实解出，同时又不能被目标 solver 设定下全部轻松解出。论文的核心是在任务合成阶段引入基于 solver 行为的多轮对抗式校准，让“任务难度是否合适”从事后经验判断，变成可观测、可操作、可迭代优化的构造目标。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在训练数据构造，而不是 agent 应用本身。它给出的关键信号是：对于可验证环境任务，数据质量不应只看正确性和多样性，还应看相对于训练中模型能力的“可学习性带宽”。这和预训练里的 data curriculum、difficulty shaping、self-play data generation 有直接对应关系。论文贡献的量级不在新模型结构，而在提出了一种更细粒度的数据筛选与修订机制：不是简单保留 solved/unsolved 样本，而是利用强弱 solver 的差异、失败轨迹和多轮重写，把任务推向更有监督密度的区域。对做代码 agent、tool-use、terminal interaction 数据合成的人，这个思路是实用的；对更广义的 pretrain/post-train 接口研究，它也提示了一个方向：训练数据的价值取决于模型相对能力，而不是静态标签质量。","technical_approach":"方法上，CalibForge 是一个从 clue 出发的自动任务合成系统，但真正的创新不在 authoring pipeline 本身，而在后面的 adversarial solver calibration。流程大致分四步。第一步，authoring agent 在 draft sandbox 中做外部调研，查官方文档、GitHub issue、Stack Overflow 等，寻找具体且可复现的工程问题，例如版本冲突、配置陷阱、依赖 bug、边界条件。第二步，它联合生成任务说明、执行环境和验证测试，并做结构校验与 self-solving，确保候选任务至少是可执行、可验证、可被系统自身完成的。第三步，进入校准环节。论文给了两种校准准则。multi-solver calibration 追求异构 solver 池中的分歧，即至少一个成功、至少一个失败；contrastive solver calibration 则更强，要求指定的强 solver 通过、弱 solver 失败。两者都把“可学区间”定义成 solver 相对关系，而不是绝对难度分数。第四步，如果当前候选不满足目标行为模式，authoring agent 不只是微调 wording，而是可以回到外部研究、重写任务方向、修改环境、测试和约束，再重新验证和 probing，直到满足条件或达到最大轮数。\n\n这里有两个值得注意的设计。其一，反馈信号不只用 pass/fail，还用完整 trajectory。这样 authoring agent 能根据失败原因去改任务，例如减少偶然性、强化目标约束、修正环境噪声，或者反过来引入更能区分强弱 solver 的关键步骤。其二，论文强调“constrained adversarial”：作者代理在对抗地改任务，但 retention criterion 始终要求至少存在真实成功解，因此不会把任务推向纯粹刁钻或不可解的方向。和只用单一 solver 打分、或者只做一次 authoring+validation 的方法相比，这里多了一层基于行为证据的闭环修订。这个区别很重要，因为 learnability 不是任务初稿的静态属性，而是可以通过重写被主动塑形的。","experiments":"$2e","reading_guide":"如果只花一小时，先看 Introduction 里对 learnable zone 的定义，再看 Methodology 中 Figure 2、Algorithm 1，把 author–solver loop 的控制流理清。随后重点看两类 calibration criterion：multi-solver 和 contrastive。这里决定了任务被保留的逻辑，也决定了这篇工作的真正贡献不只是“自动出题”，而是“按 solver 相对能力塑形数据”。\n\n实验部分建议先抓三张信息最密的结果：一是初始候选满足 contrastive relation 的比例与多轮修订后的累计接受率；二是不同数据构造策略在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上的对比；三是用 5,431 个任务训练后的 30B/35B 结果及其在 SWE-bench Pro、Doc2Repo 上的迁移表现。读到这里，基本就能判断这套方法是否只是 task-generation masked/AR 预训练中的 difficulty-aware sampling。若做复现，先不要急着复刻全系统，先验证“多 solver 分歧筛选”相对“单 solver 通过率筛选”的增益是否稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not mainly about generating executable terminal tasks. It targets a harder problem: how to synthesize tasks that are learnable for a given solver regime, rather than merely valid, trivial, or effectively impossible. Executable validation only tells you that a task can run and be checked. It does not tell you whether the task sits in a useful difficulty band for training. CalibForge formalizes this as a solver-relative learnable zone: a retained task must be demonstrably solvable by at least one solver, yet not uniformly solved under the target solver setting. deri","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06861.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作处理长程 LLM agent 强化学习里的稀疏奖励归因问题：终局成功或失败只给出轨迹级信号，但策略更新发生在每个动作响应上。GRPO 这类 critic-free 方法把同一个 outcome advantage 分配给整条轨迹，容易奖励成功轨迹里的无效动作，也会惩罚失败轨迹里的有用动作。已有 step-level 方法尝试用重复状态或历史匹配构造局部比较，但对 rollout 之间共享的转移结构利用不足，也缺少一个机制来判断局部 step credit 是否有足够证据支撑。","importance":"对预训练方向的直接贡献有限，但对 post-train 中的 agentic RL 有参考价值，尤其是 critic-free credit assignment。它的启发点在于：不训练独立 critic，也可以从同一任务的多条 rollout 中构造一个经验状态图，用图上的 Bellman evaluation 产生更细的步骤级训练信号。这类方法适合用于成本敏感的 agent RL，因为 learned critic 在长程环境里既贵又不稳定。对基座研究员更有用的是训练信号质量的处理思路：何时相信局部估计，何时回退到全局 outcome，是比单纯设计 advantage 公式更关键的问题。","technical_approach":"Gated-BEPO 对每个任务实例采样 K 条 rollout，并把环境观察转成可哈希的状态 key。它将相同观察合并成经验状态图，图边来自 rollout records，即状态、动作、奖励、下一状态。与 GraphGPO 使用到成功终点的最短路径距离不同，Gated-BEPO 在经验图上做 mean-backup Bellman fixed-point evaluation，用当前 rollout 图估计节点 value。随后计算 fixed-point TD residual，并沿每条轨迹用 GAE 传播，得到 step-level advantage。\n\n关键设计是 confidence gate。只有当某个状态在 rollout group 中出现多个 observed successors 时，局部图才提供了比较不同后继转移的证据。此时方法激活 Bellman step credit，并提高它在总 advantage 中的权重；如果状态没有分叉，局部估计没有可比较的经验依据，Bellman credit 会被抑制，更新主要依赖 group-relative outcome advantage。这样做避免了两个问题：一是无证据的局部 value 估计把噪声注入更新；二是有证据的步骤级 credit 被粗粒度 outcome 信号淹没。\n\n它和几类相关方法的差异比较清楚。GRPO 只做轨迹级归因；GiGPO/HGPO 依赖状态或历史匹配来形成 step group；GAGPO 使用 grouped return proxy 再做 TD/GAE 风格传播；GraphGPO 合并状态后用到成功终点的图距离构造 value，可能受 perceptual aliasing 影响。Gated-BEPO 的核心变化是把经验图当作当前策略下的有限样本 MDP 来评估，并用分叉证据决定是否采用局部 credit。","experiments":"实验覆盖 WebShop、ALFWorld 和 visual Sokoban，包含文本 agent 与视觉语言 agent 设置。文中提到使用 Qwen2.5 系列 text 和 vision-language gate、以及 outcome-step balanced fusion。结果含义是，单独依赖图上 Bellman credit 会引入不可靠局部估计，单独依赖 outcome credit 又过于粗糙；gate 的作用不是简单调参，而是在局部比较证据存在时才让 step credit 参与训练。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。先看 Figure 2 和 backbone 上。需要特别留意状态合并策略：文本环境用 observation key，视觉环境用图像状态，这会影响 perceptual aliasing 和图上 value 的可靠性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work addresses sparse-reward credit assignment for long-horizon LLM agents. A terminal success or failure label is observed only at the trajectory level, while policy updates are applied to individual action responses. Critic-free methods such as GRPO assign the same outcome-derived advantage to every step, which can reinforce unhelpful actions in successful trajectories and penalize useful actions in failed ones. Existing step-level methods use repeated states or history matching, but they do not fully exploit the shared transition structure across rollouts and do not decide when local step credit is empirically supported.","importance":"The paper is more relevant to agentic post-training than to base pretraining, but it provides a useful training-signal design pattern. It shows how a critic-free method can recover finer-grained credit from multiple rollouts of the same task by building an empirical state graph, rather than learning a separate critic. This matters for long-horizon agent RL, where learned critics are expensive and often unstable. The most useful idea is the confidence mechanism: local value estimates should affect policy updates only when the rollout group contains enough branching evidence; otherwise the method should fall back to the more stable trajectory-level outcome signal.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"The experiments cover WebShop, ALFWorld, and visual Sokoban, including both text-only and vision-language agent settings. Gated-BEPO improves success rate over GRPO and other step-credit baselines, with gains up to 4.1% over the strongest competing method.\n\nAblations indicate that the Bellman fixed-point signal, the confidence gate, and the balanced fusion of outcome and step-level credit are all important. The takeaway is that Bellman credit alone can be noisy when the local graph lacks branching evidence, while outcome credit alone remains too coarse. The gate is therefore not just a weighting heuristic; it controls whether the local graph contains enough evidence to influence the update.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Existing RLVR ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_07147.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在 coding agent 的 RLVR 训练中，单步动作往往对应一个包含多处修改的 code diff，导致 credit assignment 过粗：成功/失败的回报无法区分 diff 内不同子修改的贡献。论文要解决的是：不引入 critic、也不增加额外环境 rollouts 的情况下，如何把轨迹级回报细化到 diff 内部的子区域，并把更细粒度的优势信号回传到对应的响应 critic 的细粒度 credit 构造方式，和 GRPO/DAPO/GSPO 这类 group-based policy optimization 的工程路线兼容，适合在大规模 RLVR 训练里控制不稳定性与成本。\n\n是否值得投入取决于你的工作是否覆盖 coding agent 的 RL 训练；若主要做基座预训练/数据/优化，这篇更多是“了解一种 credit 细化的做法”，不太会直接改你的 pretrain recipe。","technical_approach":"DiDPO 的核心是把“轨迹级 group-relative advantage”继续向下分解到 diff 内部。\n\n设定上沿用 CodeAct 式 Thought-Action-Observation 循环：每步 action 产生一次代码编辑（add/del/none），环境给 outcome reward（例如测试是否通过）。传统做法把整段响应当作一个 credit 载体；GiGPO 会按相似 state/action 分组做 state-level advantage，但 coding 的关键困难是：同一步 diff 里可能包含多个功能区域的修改。\n\nDiDPO 做了三件事：\n1）跨 rollouts 对齐 diff：对同一任务 prompt 采样多条轨迹，比较相邻版本之间的 diffs，在不同 rollout 的 diffs 中寻找“高度相似的 sub-diff”作为 anchor。\n2）用 anchor 诱导 sub-diff 边界：把一个大 diff 切成若干 sub-diff，使这些 sub-diff 更像可复用的功能单元（例如某个函数、某段逻辑、某处 import/配置）。\n3）sub-diff 分组并计算 diff-level advantage：把对齐后的 sub-diff 组成局部 group，计算局部的 group-relative advantage（diff-level advantage），再与轨迹级 advantage 结合，并投影回生成这些 sub-diff 的 token 上更新策略。\n\n为避免 anchor 过大（语义太杂）或过小（难以聚合成组），论文引入 groupability score 来选择 anchor，平衡语义覆盖与可形成足够样本量的分组。\n\n整体上它是 critic-free 的：优势来自组内相对比较，而不是学习一个 value function；并且强调“不需要额外 rollouts”，即在同一批采样轨迹上做更细的对齐与分配。","experiments":"论文声称在多个 long-horizon coding We introduce TRIAL, a trajectory-relative hindsight distillation framework with a unified turn-aligned scoring protocol. For each decision turn, TRIAL extracts an outcome view of that decision's realized consequence and evaluates the same response under ordinary and hindsight-conditio","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_07371.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"TRIAL 解决的是 agentic RL 中稀疏结果奖励和多轮决策之间的信用分配问题。一次完整 rollout 只有最终成功或失败信号，但轨迹里包含多个决策 turn。已有 hindsight distillation 能给每个已执行动作构造事后监督，却没有清楚回答一个问题：这些 dense hindsight 信号应该在不同 turn 之间如何分配强度。","importance":"这项工作和预训练训练流程的关系较间接，但对 RL 后训练、长程 agent 训练和 verifier/reward 信号设计有启发。它把 hindsight 信号拆成两个层次：token 级别的方向由 log-prob gap 决定，turn 级别的权重由整条轨迹内的相对 revision magnitude 决定。这个设计比简单给所有 hindsight token 同权更稳，因为它控制了平均权重，同时允许关键 turn 获得更大训练强度。对需要把长程交互数据转成可训练监督信号的系统，这是一种可复用的 credit allocation recipe。","technical_approach":"TRIAL 保留 GRPO 的 rollout-level outcome advantage 和 clipping 规则，在此基础上加入一个训练时 hindsight objective。对每个决策 turn，方法构造一个 outcome view，把该决策实际导致的局部后果暴露给 scorer。然后用同一个已生成 response，在 ordinary context 和 hindsight-conditioned context 下分别计算 token log probability。两者的有符号差值决定 token 更新方向和局部强度。接着，TRIAL 汇总每个 turn 内 eligible tokens 的绝对 gap，并和该 turn 在整条轨迹中的 eligible-token 占比比较，得到 trajectory-relative allocation multiplier。这些 multiplier 的 eligible-token 加权平均值被归一为 1，因此它是在固定平均训练强度下重新分配 hindsight 监督。最后，dense hindsight loss 与 GRPO loss 结合，并用相对 detached GRPO loss magnitude 的 scalar clamp 控制 dense 项强度。所有 hindsight context 和分配 profile 只在训练时使用，部署时不增加推理开销。","experiments":"实验在 WebShop 和 ALFWorld 上进行，backbone 包括 Qwen2.5-3B 和 Qwen3-1.7B。TRIAL 在 backbone、环境和指标组成的 8 个组合中都优于 GRPO；和 5 个 baseline 比较时，在 6 个 case 中最好或并列最好。WebShop 上使用 Qwen3-1.7B 时，success rate 从 GRPO 的 56.4% 提升到 75.2%，task score 从 78.7% 提升到 85.7%。消融实验固定 hindsight pathway，只替换 turn allocation profile。trajectory-relative profile 在 WebShop 和 ALFWorld 的 4 个指标上都优于 unit profile，说明收益不只是来自 dense hindsight distillation，还来自轨迹内相对权重分配。source-shuffled profile 的对照也支持 profile 和真实轨迹结构相关，而不是简单噪声正则化。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。先看 Method 中对 signed token gap、eligible-token mask、turn-level magnitude 和 trajectory-normalized multiplier 的定义；这是全文核心。再看 Figure 2，理解 GRPO outcome supervision 和 TRIAL hindsight supervision 如何并行工作。实验部分重点看 WebShop/Qwen3-1.7B 的提升，以及 unit profile、source-shuffled profile 的消融。若关注 RL 训练稳定性，可以额外看 dense loss clamp 的处理方式，它反映了作者在不破坏 GRPO advantage construction 的前提下加入辅助信号的取舍。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"TRIAL addresses credit allocation in agentic reinforcement learning. A multi-turn rollout usually receives a sparse final outcome reward, but the trajectory contains many decisions made under different observations. Hindsight distillation can provide dense supervision for realized actions, yet it leaves open how much of that supervision should be assigned to each turn.","importance":"The work is most relevant to RL post-training and long-horizon agent training rather than pretraining itself. Its useful idea is to separate token-level revision direction from turn-level allocation. The signed log-probability gap gives local token supervision, while a trajectory-relative profile decides which turns deserve more weight. This is a practical recipe for converting long interaction traces into calibrated training signals without simply giving every hindsight token the same weight.","technical_approach":"$34","experiments":"The experiments use WebShop and ALFWorld with Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen3-1.7B. TRIAL improves over GRPO in all eight combinations of model, environment, and metric, and is best or tied best among six methods in six cases. On WebShop with Qwen3-1.7B, success rate increases from 56.4% to 75.2%, and task score increases from 78.7% to 85.7%. Controlled ablations keep the hindsight pathway fixed and vary only the allocation profile. The trajectory-relative profile outperforms a unit profile on all four WebShop and ALFWorld metrics, showing that the gains are not just from dense hindsight supervision. The source-shuffled profile comparison further suggests that the allocation profile benefits from being aligned with the actual trajectory structure.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is appropriate. for large-scale training, largely on the basis of faster convergence and favorable spectral properties. This paper shows that faster grokking does not imply stable retention of the solution, and localizes the failure to a basis-freedom issue in the residual stream: when hidden layers are updated by one optimizer while embeddings and the unembedding are updated by another, they can drift along invertible directions to which the loss is insensitive, eventually breaking coordination between representations and the readout. DLLMs initialized from AR predecessors directly inherit the mechanistic safety footprint of their source models, with no fundamental restructuring of safety representations from diffusion training. This finding has direct implications for alignment design and safety evaluation of non-AR language models, showing that a shift away from next-token prediction does not automatically eliminate known alignment failure modes from AR systems.","technical_approach":"First identifies sparse safety neurons driving refusal behavior in both native DLLMs (e.g., LLaDA) and AR-initialized DLLMs (e.g., Dream, Fast-dLLM) via activation profiling and logistic regression, and measures neuron overlap between AR source models and their diffusion derivatives. A weighted Safety Neuron (SN) loss is constructed from identified neuron activations and corresponding regression coefficients, serving as a continuous signal to distinguish safe and unsafe activation regions. The bidirectional denoising process of DLLMs is then used to run fully offline inference-time adversarial optimization: a Generative Pruning Cascade uses surrogate DLLMs with progressively relaxed neuron pruning to iteratively refine malicious prompts and filter semantic drift or degeneration, before SN-guidance steers denoising trajectories away from safety-triggering regions, requiring no iterative black-box queries to target models or RL fine-tuning.","experiments":"Self-pruning of identified safety neurons raises attack success rate (ASR) from 2.6% to 73.8% on LLaDA. capt","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06938.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般的视觉理解错误，而是 MLLM 在“视觉证据与语言常识先验冲突”时，为什么会系统性地选错。作者先做了一个很关键的归因：问题主要不在视觉编码器没看到，而在共享语言解码器的决策策略。模型往往已经把正确视觉证据编码进了内部表征，甚至正确答案也在解码空间里，但一旦遇到低频、反常识事实，解码器会被高频语言先验牵着走，输出更“像常识”的答案。论文因此把问题重新表述为：如何在不额外增强视觉监督的前提下，重塑解码器在 prior–evidence conflict 下的选择偏好，让模型先跟随证据，再做推理。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它给多模态错误归因提供了一个更细的视角：不少所谓“视觉 hallucination”并不是 perception failure，而是 decoder policy failure。这一点对预训练和后训练接口都很重要，因为它意味着某些多模态推理缺陷可以通过语言侧监督去修，而不一定要回到视觉编码器或跨模态对齐层重训。第二，它提出了一种 text-anchored 的跨模态迁移思路：如果错误根源在共享语言解码器，那么只用文本构造的反常识监督，也可能迁移到视觉场景中的证据跟随行为。这种思路对 post-train 反哺 pretrain、对齐数据如何改变模型决策边界，都有直接启发。量级上，这不是在改基座架构，而是在给“多模态推理偏差来自哪里、该怎么修”提供一个较完整的方法链条和实验论证。","technical_approach":"方法分两部分：数据构造 FFD 和两阶段后训练 TACT。\n\n先看 FFD。作者不是直接从图像里挖 counter-commonsense 样本，而是先做 text-only 的事实蒸馏。核心想法是估计一个事实在语料中的“频率强度”，把高频常识事实和低频反常识事实区分开，再围绕这些冲突事实构造高质量 QA。这里的关键不是普通数据合成，而是 frequency-aware routing：模型先识别哪些事实容易触发强先验，再优先蒸馏那些信息量高、能形成 prior–evidence 对抗的样本。这样得到的不是泛泛的问答，而是专门针对“语言先验压过证据”这一失效模式的数据。\n\n再看 TACT。它建立在一个判断上：既然正确答案已经在解码空间里，训练目标就不该只是让模型学会答案，而要让模型学会“沿着证据轨迹走”。因此作者做了 prior-aware trajectory curation，对生成的推理轨迹进行筛选，区分 evidence-following 和 prior-driven 的 reasoning path。随后用两阶段 post-training 去优化共享语言解码器：第一阶段更像监督微调，显式强化先描述观察、再做判断的行为；第二阶段引入偏好学习，进一步压制那些虽然语言上流畅、但实际是在为错误先验找理由的 over-rationalization 轨迹。论文反复强调，这个过程不依赖新增视觉监督，依赖的是共享 decoder 的跨模态迁移能力。\n\n和已有方法相比，差异很清楚。VCD、VDD、M3ID、MemVR 这类方法主要在视觉输入或视觉对比解码上做文章，默认错误来自视觉 grounding 不足；NoLan 一类方法更像 inference-time calibration。TACT 则把重点放在训练期重塑 decoder policy，而且用文本锚定的数据和轨迹监督去做。换句话说，它不是增强“看见什么”，而是增强“当看见的东西和常识冲突时，愿不愿意相信自己看见的”。","experiments":"最有信息量的实验其实是前面的归因实验。作者在 CDH-Bench 上对三个 MLLM 做 multi-trajectory decoding 分析，设 n=8、temperature=1.0，考察 pass@k 和自定义的 prior-lock@k。结果显示，当 k 从 1 增加到 8 时，pass@k 提升 15 到 22 个点，同时 prior-lock@k 下降 14 到 18 个点。这个结果很关键，因为它说明正确答案并非不可达，而是被先验压制。对 Qwen3-VL-8B 的错误分析也支持这一点：大量错误都落在 commonsense option 上，而一个简单的 grounding prompt——先要求描述相关视觉细节——就能明显减少错误。\n\n主实验部分，作者在多个 visual counter-commonsense benchmark 上验证 TACT，结论是能稳定提升这类推理，同时尽量保持通用能力不掉。摘要和正文给出的核心卖点是：只用 text-only supervision，就能改善视觉 counter-commonsense reasoning。你真正要看的是提升是否集中在 prior-conflict 样本、是否对一般 VQA/通用多模态能力影响有限，以及两阶段训练各自贡献多少。文中还做了方法拆解：FFD 数据构造、trajectory curation、两阶段训练缺一不可；如果只做普通 SFT 或只做答案级监督，收益会明显弱于带 prior-aware 轨迹筛选的版本。另一个值得注意的点是 over-rationalization 分析：有些错误不是没看到，而是 CoT 会主动编造一条能解释错误先验的视觉叙述，这说明仅靠增加 CoT 长度并不能解决问题，反而可能放大错误解释的自洽性。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三处。\n\n第一，看 Introduction 和 Preliminary 里的归因逻辑，尤其是 Figure 2、pass@k 与 prior-driven。这里关系到方法是否真在打击目标失效模式，而不是做了一个更花哨的数据合成流程。\n\n第三，看实验中的 ablation 和 failure case。重点关注：只用文本监督为何能迁移到视觉推理；两阶段训练分别带来什么；通用能力是否受损；哪些错误仍然无法修复。预计 30 到 45 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。如果要深挖，建议把 Appendix 里错误案例、grounding prompt 分析和 Qwen3-VL-8B 的 failure taxonomy 一起看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode in MLLMs: when visual evidence conflicts with dominant language priors, models often answer with the prior-consistent option instead of the visually supported one. The key claim is that this is not mainly a perception bottleneck. The relevant visual evidence is often already encoded, and the correct answer is reachable in decoding. The failure comes from the shared language decoder, which resolves prior–evidence conflicts by favoring high-frequency commonsense continuations. The problem is therefore framed as reshaping the decoder’s decision policy so that it follows evidence under conflict, without requiring additional visual supervision.","importance":"Its value lies less in raw benchmark gains and more in the diagnosis. The paper argues that a nontrivial portion of multimodal reasoning errors should be understood as decoder-policy errors rather than visual-grounding errors. That matters for pretrain and post-train research because it changes where one would intervene: not necessarily in the vision encoder or cross-modal connector, but in the language-side supervision that governs conflict resolution. The second contribution is methodological: if the bottleneck lives in the shared decoder, then text-only supervision can transfer to visual reasoning behavior. That is a useful idea for the pretrain–posttrain interface, especially for studying how targeted supervision changes model preferences under ambiguous or adversarial evidence.","technical_approach":"$37","experiments":"$38","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three parts.\n\nFirst, read the diagnosis in the introduction and preliminary sections, especially the pass@k and prior-lock@k analysis around Figure 2. If that argument is not convincing, the rest is much less interesting.\n\nSecond, read the method section with one question in mind: how exactly does the pipeline isolate prior-sensitive facts and curate evidence-following trajectories? The names FFD and TACT matter less than the operational details behind frequency estimation, routing, and trajectory filtering.\n\nThird, check the ablations and failure cases. The useful questions are: why does text-only supervision transfer to visual reasoning, how much does each training stage contribute, and what failure modes remain after training? mechanistic interpretability 里的一个老问题：我们到底是在解释模型内部真正起作用的权重机制，还是只是在解释一个外部训练出来的代理字典。稀疏自编码器这类方法能给激活方向贴标签，但“哪个权重规则把这个方向写进 residual stream”往往是隐含的。作者试图直接从模型已有线性权重矩阵里抽取可用的 mechanism unit，而不是再训练一个代理表示。更具体地说，他们想回答：把一个线性映射按列分块后做局部 SVD，得到的奇异模式，是否比整矩阵 SVD 或简单列采样更接近模型在真实分布上使用的写入机制。","importance":"和预训练研究的直接关系较弱，更偏机制分析工具。但它有两个可能的间接启发。第一，它强调“解释对象应尽量贴近真实权重路径”，这对分析预训练后模型内部表征、残差写入、MLP/attention 子路径的功能分解是有帮助的。第二，它提出了一套偏工程化的可用性判据，不只看某个方向是否可解释，而是看它是否在真实前向中承载写入能量、是否覆盖权重、是否能通过残差注入影响最终 logits。对做训练动态和内部机制分析的人，这种评价口径比只看可视化或 token cluster 更扎实一些。不过它不是基础模型训练方法，也没有展示对模型能力或训练效率的反馈，因此只能算边缘相关。","technical_approach":"作者提出的是 column-tiled SVD。给定某个线性层权重 W，不直接对整个 W 做一次全局 SVD，而是把输入列按 tile 切块，对每个 tile 单独做低秩分解，从中抽取若干 mode。每个 mode 被定义为一个 mount=(v,u,σ)：v 是 trigger 方向，u 是 write 方向，σ 是强度。作者强调 mount 的身份就是权重规则本身，而不是后验命名的概念标签。\n\n方法的关键不在分解本身，而在评价标准。文章明确反对用 tile-local lift 这类指标，因为单列 tile 会天然占优，无法公平比较不同 chunking。于是他们设计了三类检查。A 类看 full-write energy lift：在真实前向输入 x 上，用 mount 诱导的写入方向去解释整个站点的写入张量 Δh，而不是只解释 tile 内局部输出。B 类看 coverage 和 saturation：包括权重 Frobenius 能量覆盖、稀疏写入覆盖、相对随机字典的 coverage lift，以及随着每 tile mode 数增加是否很快饱和。C 类只用于 residual write 站点，检查这些 write 方向是否能通过 post-norm 残差注入，在最终 unembedding 几何上产生可检测的 logit 变化；指标是 Spearman ρ 和 top-20 Jaccard。\n\n另一个重要细节是 effective-path mounts。作者发现对 mlp.up 和 attn.v，直接拿原始模块权重做 residual-shaped 评价会失败，因为模型在真实分布上使用的并不是裸矩阵。于是对 mlp.up 用平均 gate 激活构造 W*=diag(ḡ)W_up，对 attn.v 用 ridge least squares 拟合到 mixed-v，必要时再用与 o_proj 组合的近似路径。也就是说，这篇文章的一个核心主张是：若解释对象选错了路径，再好的分解也会显得“不工作”；要先贴近 on-distribution 的有效计算图，再谈机制抽取。","experiments":"实验模型是 Gemma-2-2B，共 26 层，每层考察 7 个线性映射，总计 182 个 site-layer。数据来自 WikiText-2 raw train，先收集 86,109 个 token，再固定随机种子下抽样 16,384 个 token 做分析。默认 tile 宽度按站点区分：残差写入和 MLP 映射 T=512，attn.k/attn.v 为 256，attn.q 为 128，困难层可退到 64。A/C 实验默认每 tile 取 k=2 个 mode，B 实验扫 k∈{1,2,4,8,16}。\n\n主结果是：在采用 site-aware tile size 和 effective-path 处理后，7 个站点 × 26 层全部通过预设判据，即 182/182 通过；其中 residual write 站点需要同时通过 A/B/C，共 52/52，其他站点通过 A/B，共 130/130。对两个 residual-write 站点，tile SVD 在几乎所有深度上都优于 whole-matrix SVD、列采样和随机基线，只有 mlp.down 第 25 层出现 tile≈whole，但按作者的 A4 ratio 规则仍算通过。文章还指出，column sampling 在 tile-local 指标下会出现约 0.999 的虚高结果，因此被排除出主判据。\n\n覆盖实验显示，在 residual write 站点上，mode 数 m=1 或 2 时 coverage lift 已经很高，之后基本变平，说明额外 mode 主要带来冗余而不是新的有效机制。对 residual write 的 steering 检查，作者采用在 post-attention 或 post-FF 的 RMSNorm 后注入 αu，α=2，最多在 8 段文本上平均最后一个 token 的 logit 变化，并与 unembed lens 预测比较。通过阈值设为 ρ≥0.05 或 J20≥0.05，且只对第 6 层及以后强制要求。整体结论是，局部列结构比全局单次 SVD 更能恢复真实分布上的高能写入方向。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，理解作者在批评什么：不是说 SAE 没用，而是说 SAE 解释的是 proxy space，不一定是权重机制本身。然后直接看 Method 里 mount 的定义、A/B/C 三类判据，以及 mlp.up、attn.v 的 effective-path 处理。这几处决定了文章是否成立。\n\n实验部分建议重点看三个点。第一，残差写入站点上 tile SVD 与 whole SVD、column sampling 的比较，这是方法是否有必要的直接证据。第二，coverage saturation 曲线，作者借此论证每 tile 少量 mode 就够，更多 mode 多半是冗余。第三，steer check 的设计细节：为什么要在 post-norm 残差位置注入，而不是直接改 o_proj/down_proj 输出，因为作者想测的是“这个方向写进 residual stream 后是否沿最终 logit 几何起作用”。\n\n如果只想判断这篇文章值不值得深读，40 分钟足够。若要复现或借鉴其分析框架，需要更仔细看判据定义和 effective-path fallback 细节。值得保留一点审慎：全文主要在单模型 Gemma-2-2B、单语料子样本上验证，阈值和通过标准也带有较强方法学设定，泛化到更大模型或不同架构还需要额外证据。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a recurring issue in mechanistic interpretability: are we explaining the model’s actual weight-level mechanisms, or only a proxy dictionary learned on activations? Sparse autoencoders and related methods can produce useful feature atlases, but they do not directly identify the weight rule that writes into the residual stream. The authors propose extracting mechanism units directly from existing linear maps. The concrete question is whether column-wise tiled SVD yields more usable on-distribution mechanisms than whole-matrix SVD or simple column sampling.","importance":"This is only loosely connected to pretraining, but it is relevant to internal analysis of pretrained models. Agents often rely on external state, tools, memories, trackers, and logs, but the policy for when to read, write, update, or consolidate this support is usually hand-designed through prompts or heuristics. EvoHarness-RL trains the model to coordinate with the harness at runtime.","importance":"This is not a base pretraining paper. Its relevance is mainly to agent post-training, RL for tool and memory use, and long-horizon execution. The useful idea is to make external-state access part of the learned policy and to price that access explicitly. For pretraining researchers, it suggests that long-horizon capability may depend not only on longer context or more trajectory imitation, but also on whether models learn when to externalize state, when to rely on internal computation, and when to reuse prior experience.","technical_approach":"EvoHarness-RL abstracts heterogeneous runtime support into a BPE workspace. Belief tracks the current environment state, Progress tracks completed and pending subgoals, and Experience stores reusable cross-episode knowledge. The agent interacts with this workspace through compact harness meta-actions, such as querying belief, committing progress, recalling experience, and writing new insights. Training has two stages. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the model the BPE action protocol and how to extract useful belief, progress, and experience from traces. Cost-aware GRPO then optimizes when to use the harness under an interaction budget. The reward includes task success, efficiency, action diversity, repetition avoidance, and valid action formatting. The main contribution is turning harness use from a prompt-time scaffold into a learned runtime control policy.","experiments":"The framework is instantiated in ALFWorld. In this environment, Belief is grounded as a world-state tracker, Progress as a committed subgoal plan, and Experience as a cross-episode skill bank for household tasks. Prompt-time BPE already improves stateful long-horizon execution. After SFT and GRPO, the reported success rate reaches 96.9% on the ALFWorld seen split and 86.6% on the unseen split. The analysis highlights two dynamics. Harness annealing means that training reduces frequent scaffold-like calls and shifts the agent toward selective external-state access. Harness evolution means that online progress updates and experience consolidation refine the external workspace into a more compact task-adaptive substrate.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20–30 minutes. This reward misalignment causes agents to form conclusions from internal parametric priors, using retrieval only to confirm pre-existing hypotheses, leading to confirmation bias and low utilization of retrieved information.","importance":"The annotation-free dense reward design provides practical reference for post-training of tool-augmented LLMs, addressing the common 'retrieve but not use' failure mode in search agent systems. The signal requires no separate reward model, human labels, or additional rollouts, keeping implementation overhead low.","technical_approach":"Proposes Contextual Information Policy Optimization (CIPO), an evidence-oriented RL framework. Its core signal is the Evidence-Access Log-Likelihood Ratio (EALR): for each reasoning step after a retrieval call, all preceding trajectory context is held fixed, and the log-likelihood of the current action is computed under two conditions — with retrieved evidence visible, and with retrieved evidence masked. The difference between the two likelihoods directly quantifies how sensitive the current step is to the newly retrieved content. EALR serves as a per-step dense reward, combined with global outcome rewards for final answer correctness during policy optimization, to penalize steps that rely solely on internal priors without incorporating retrieved evidence.","experiments":"Evaluated across 7 in-domain and out-of-domain knowledge-intensive benchmarks, CIPO substantially reduces the rate of prior-driven reasoning, and outperforms existing RL search agent baselines on most tasks.","reading_guide":"Skim for the core reward design idea. live-web agent 评测几乎都只看最终答案或任务完成率，忽略了执行过程中的策略约束：要核验哪些证据、遇到不确定性时是否该追问、哪些偏好必须进入排序、什么时候应该停止而不是继续猜。问题的难点在于，这类委托策略不是静态标签，而是会贯穿浏览、点击、检索、澄清、停止、回答整个闭环；而且 live web 页面持续变化，最终答案即便看起来合理，也可能是在违反用户 policy post-train、tool-use、web agent 数据合成的人，这意味着需要把可验证的中间控制状态、证据义务和 stopping policy 显式写进训练对象，而不是只收集问答对。对 pretrain 研究员的启发主要在接口层：如果未来想把 agentic behavior 更早地纳入 continual pretrain 或 作为局部监督目标，中层 policy 可以在固定顶层 controller、guard 和 executor 的条件下单独训练与替换。这说明作者不是只提出一个评测框架，而是在尝试把 agent 过程控制切成可学习、可审计的模块。","reading_guide":"如果只是泛读，先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，抓住论文真正要解决的不是网页导航成功率，而是 delegation Current live-web evaluation mostly scores final answers or completion, which misses a central failure mode: the agent may produce a plausible answer after ignoring the user’s verification requirements, uncertainty policy, ranking preferences, or stopping criteria. This is especially problematic on the live web, where evidence arrives incrementally, pages change, and browsing, grounding, clarification, and stopping are tightly coupled. The paper formalizes this missing object as an intent contract and asks a sharper question: how can an agent maintain contract fidelity at every visible step, rather than merely ending with an acceptable-looking answer?","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, and its relevance to foundation-model pretraining is indirect. That gap means many trajectories that look successful at the endpoint are actually bad supervision if one cares about delegated behavior. For post-training, tool-use, and web-agent data generation, the implication is clear: intermediate control state, evidence obligations, and stopping policy need to be explicit training targets. This leads to representation mismatch, where relevant information is available but not organized for the current decision. To this end, we propose MemPrism, a task-conditioned relational memory framework that separates persistent experience storage from decision-time working memory. MemPrism records interactions as the event stre","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_06745.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的是长程 agent memory 里的一个更细的失败类型：信息已经被存下来了，也被取回来了，但呈现方式不适合当前决策，因此 agent 仍然用不好。作者把这个问题称为 representation mismatch，并把它和更常见的 storage failure、retrieval failure 区分开。核心问题不是“记忆有没有”，而是“同一段历史在当前子任务下应该以什么关系结构被组织出来”。例如，检测循环需要把重复动作和结果对齐，状态跟踪需要按实体聚合同一对象的变化，失败分析需要把动作和环境反馈连起来，依赖分析则需要把相隔很远的事件串起来。固定的时间线、摘要或向量检索结果，往往只能支持其中一类关系，不能覆盖所有决策需求。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接影响有限，因为这不是基座模型训练论文；但它对 agent memory 的接口设计提出了一个有价值的拆分：持久记忆应保存稳定事实，工作记忆应按当前任务临时构造。这个拆分对做长上下文、memory-augmented LM、tool-use agent 的人都有启发。很多系统把 memory 问题理解成“怎么压缩、怎么检索、怎么写回”，这篇论文强调还差一步：检索之后如何重排成当前策略真正可消费的结构。若把这个观点放到更广的训练视角，它提示我们，模型性能不只受知识覆盖影响，也受输入组织形式影响；同样的证据，换一种结构化呈现，决策质量可能明显不同。","technical_approach":"MemPrism 的方法核心是把 persistent memory 和 decision-time working memory 明确分开。系统由五个模块组成：Recorder、Router、Composer、Render、Adapter。推理时主要用前四个模块。Recorder 把真实交互记录成统一 event stream，既保留原始事件，也保留结构化字段，如动作前观察、执行动作、环境反馈、奖励或步骤标签、任务元信息等。这里的设计意图是让持久层尽量保持中性和可复用，不提前把历史压缩成某一种固定摘要。\n\nRouter 是一个轻量的 view policy，它不直接输出环境动作，而是先选择“该怎么看历史”。作者把 view action 定义成几个维度的组合：relation type、evidence range、outcome condition、granularity。也就是，当前该构造哪种关系视图、看多长的历史、是否只看成功/失败/特定结果、以及细节粒度多粗。这个设计把 memory read-time 的表示选择显式化了。\n\nComposer 根据 Router 的选择，从 event stream 中筛选事件并组织成关系结构；Render 再把这个结构渲染成 optical working-memory view。作者使用 optical view，不只是为了压缩 token，而是为了把关系直接编码进二维布局：空间邻近表示相关性，行列对齐支持状态比较，箭头表示变化或依赖，高亮强调失败点和任务相关证据。随后，一个冻结的 task policy 读取当前输入和这个临时视图，输出下一步环境动作。这个视图只服务当前一步，之后丢弃，不写回持久记忆。这样可以避免固定摘要或反复压缩带来的不可逆失真。\n\n训练上，作者给 Router 加了两阶段学习。先用 action-conditioned soft supervision 初始化 view policy，再用 trajectory-level grouped GRPO 优化。这里的目标不是直接学环境动作，而是学“在当前状态下选哪种 memory view 更有利于后续决策”。这种分离有助于把性能提升更明确地归因到 memory representation interface，而不是混杂在整个 agent policy 里。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，实验主线围绕 view selection 是否真的改善长程决策，而不是单纯比较存储容量。作者强调两类证据：一类是任务性能提升，另一类是归因实验，证明收益来自 working-memory 视图而不是别的模块。论文介绍里提到，view policy 先用软监督初始化，再用 grouped GRPO 做轨迹级优化，目的就是隔离 view selection 的作用。\n\n虽然当前提供的正文片段没有完整表格数字，但可以确定作者会比较固定表示与 task-conditioned relational views：例如只给时间线、只给摘要、只给原始检索片段，与按 relation type / evidence range / outcome filter / granularity 动态构造的视图相比，后者在 loop detection、state tracking、failure analysis、dependency reasoning 这类子任务上更合适。另一个关键实验点应是 temporary view 不写回 persistent memory 的设计：作者认为如果把临时视图再写回，会把某一步的任务偏置固化到长期记忆里，损害后续复用性，因此会通过消融展示 persistent event stream + on-demand view 比固定 summary memory 更稳。\n\n如果拿这篇论文做泛读，实验部分最该找的是三类结果：第一，Router 学到的 view action 分布是否随任务状态变化；第二，固定 view 与动态 view 的性能差距；第三，光学视图相对纯文本或扁平检索结果的增益是否来自结构显式化，而不只是 token 压缩。","reading_guide":"先看 Introduction，把作者提出的 representation mismatch 和 storage / retrieval failure 的边界弄清楚。这个定义是整篇论文成立的前提。如果不接受这个问题分解，后面的模块设计会显得像是在做另一种 memory engineering。\n\n然后重点看 Method 里的五模块流水线，尤其是 Router 的 action space。阅读时要问两个问题：为什么 view selection 要单独建模，而不是交给主 policy 自己在 prompt 里隐式完成；为什么 temporary working view 不写回 persistent memory。前者关系到可学习的 memory interface，后者关系到长期记忆是否被任务局部偏置污染。\n\n接着看 optical view 的具体例子。不要只把它理解成“把历史画成图像节省 retrieved, yet the agent still fails because the evidence is presented in the wrong form for the current decision. The authors call this representation mismatch and distinguish it from storage failure and retrieval failure. The key question is not whether memory exists, but how the same interaction history should be reorganized for different subtasks. Loop detection, state tracking, failure analysis, and dependency reasoning require different relational structures over the same events, and a single fixed representation rarely serves all of them well.","importance":"This is not a foundation-model pretraining paper, so the relevance is indirect.
