Many memory systems focus on what to store, how to compress, and how to retrieve. MemPrism argues that one more layer matters: how retrieved evidence is organized before it reaches the policy. In a broader training sense, the paper is a reminder that model performance depends not only on knowledge coverage but also on input organization. At each step, each agent receives only an encoded text summary of neighboring agents' relative phases, and outputs one of three actions (advance/stay/retard) to adjust its phase, with identical physical update rules across all conditions. Three encodings are tested, all computed from the same underlying phase distribution: low-order circular moments summarizing distribution direction and concentration, a 24-bin histogram labeled by bin centers, and an identical 24-bin histogram labeled by interval bounds. Initial random seeds are matched across conditions, a zero-coupling (K=0) control rules out engine confounds, and separate open-loop tests with fixed synthetic fields measure action response operators independent of closed-loop feedback effects.","experiments":"On GPT-5.4-mini, moment encoding achieved full synchronization (final polar order parameter r1 ~ 1) across 6/6 random seeds at positive coupling values, while both histogram encodings achieved 0/6 full synchronization. The K=0 control produced identical physical trajectories across all encodings, eliminating initial condition and engine implementation confounds. Open-loop response measurements showed moment encoding produces pure directional attraction, interval encoding produces a two-lobed response with zero net directional bias, and center encoding produces a reversed net retarding bias. for handling conflicting facts in pretraining corpora and knowledge base alignment, and inspires objective design for evidence validity judgment in long-context models.","technical_approach":"TEPA is a revocable evidence memory mechanism that assigns explicit lifecycle states to stored memories. Each observation is mapped to a keyed precedent, where the key identifies the memory topic (e.g., user preference slot, tool regime slot) and the value is the corresponding claim. Memories are split into an active set (used for ordinary retrieval) and a revoked set (retained for audit but excluded from regular retrieval). When new evidence conflicts with an active precedent under the same key, the stale precedent is automatically moved to the revoked set. A TEPA-Full variant adds pre-promotion validation for candidate precedents when executable verification is available. A phase-wise Memory Pollution Index (MPI) is defined to quantify how much worse a memory-equipped method performs relative to a no-memory baseline in a given phase.","experiments":"Evaluated across four conflict settings: controlled hidden regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, user preference updates, and the MemoryAgentBench SH-6k fact consolidation benchmark. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistic","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_07437.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM coding agent 在做开放式假设检验时，经常能写出可运行代码并得到数值结果，但会在统计方法选择、前提假设检查和结论推断上出错，导致 p 值和结论在统计上无效；现有数据分析 benchmark 没有系统评估这种“代码能跑但推断错误”的失效模式。","importance":"对预训练主线的直接启发有限，更接近特定科研 agent 能力评测与 post-training。但论文指出的问题有方法论意义：很多 agent benchmark 只验证“有没有执行出结果”，而不验证“执行的方法是否对应该问题和数据生成假设”。这一点和预训练后“模型能生成流畅回答但推理链/验证链不可靠”是同类评估缺口。对做 agent/RM/verifier、尤其是 code-and-tool-use agent 的研究者有参考价值；对纯基座预训练读者，可作为“评测必须验证过程合法性而非只看终态答案”的案例。","technical_approach":"构建 P-Bench，包含 425 个开放式假设检验任务，覆盖经济学、生物学、医学等真实科学数据。每个任务提供问题、数据集和数据描述，agent 需要在 R 执行环境中多轮交互，自行选择统计方法、运行分析、报告 p 值并给出拒绝/不拒绝原假设的结论。答案以可执行的参考分析为基础，经过领域专家审核，重点比对方法选择、p 值准确性和结论正确性，而非只看代码是否运行或报告是否流畅。在此基础上训练 Fisher-R1：先用合成生成的、带验证答案的假设检验任务做 SFT，再用基于结果正确性的 RL 训练。","experiments":"P-Bench 共 425 个任务，来源于同行评议论文和权威生物统计教学材料，答案键绑定到可执行参考分析。Fisher-R1-14B 在该 benchmark 上报告超过 GPT-5.4 与 DeepSeek-V4-Pro；论文展示的典型失效是模型识别到异常值/模型误设等警告信号，但仍选择错误的检验方法（如在高杠杆点存在时继续用线性回归而改用秩检验），从而得到显著但不可靠的 p 值。实验重点在 p 值准确性和结论正确性两个维度，而不是一般数据问答任务上的通过率。","reading_guide":"泛读。重点看引言里的失效模式例子（识别警告但做错误方法选择）、P-Bench 的任务设计与答案构造方式，以及为什么要把“方法选择 + p 值 + 结论”绑定在可执行参考分析上，而不是只让评委看自然语言报告。训练部分对纯预训练读者不是重点；若关心 agent post-training，可看合成任务生成和 outcome-grounded RL 的设计。预计阅读时间 15–20 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"LLM coding agents can often produce runnable code and numerical outputs for open-ended hypothesis-testing tasks, yet still make subtle inferential mistakes—choosing the wrong test, ignoring modeling assumptions, or drawing invalid conclusions—so the reported p-values are not statistically valid; Its broader relevance is methodological: many agent benchmarks reward executable workflows or plausible final answers without checking whether the chosen procedure is actually valid given the question and data-generating assumptions. This is the same class of evaluation gap that appears whenever fluent generation masks flawed reasoning or unverified tool use. It is useful for researchers working on agents, verifiers, reward modeling, and code-and-tool use; for pure pretraining readers it mainly serves as a concrete example that evaluation must validate procedural correctness, not just end-state answers.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces P-Bench, a benchmark of 425 open-ended hypothesis-testing tasks built from real scientific datasets across economics, biology, and medicine. For each task the agent receives a scientific question, a dataset, and a description, and must interact with an R execution environment across multiple turns to choose a statistical method, run the analysis, report a p-value, and decide whether to reject the null. Answer keys are grounded in logged executions of canonical reference analyses and audited by domain experts, scoring method choice, p-value accuracy, and conclusion correctness rather than code executability or report fluency alone. Fisher-R1 is then trained by supervised fine-tuning on synthetically generated hypothesis-testing tasks with verified keys, followed by reinforcement learning using outcome-grounded rewards.","experiments":"P-Bench contains 425 tasks drawn from peer-reviewed papers and authoritative biostatistics teaching materials, with answer keys tied to executable reference analyses. A representative failure case is that frontier agents can recognize warning signs such as high-leverage outliers but still proceed with an inappropriate test—for example, using linear regression where a rank-based test is required—producing a runnable analysis and fluent narrative with an invalid, yet statistically significant-looking, p-value.
